**Someone may object, “The forms of the Supreme Lord cannot be born.”**

**Комментарий:**

Now the faults attributed by others to the Pancaratra doctrine are removed. The opponents object, “From Vasudeva is born a jiva called Sankarsana, from him is born Pradyumna, the mind, and from him is born Aniruddha, the ego. This doctrine thus taught by the followers of Pancaratra is foolish, for those forms of the Supreme Lord cannot be born. "

## \|\| 2.2.42 \|\|

*na ca kartuh karanam \|\| 2.2.42 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Nor can an instrument (the mind) be born from an agent (Sankarsana).”**

**Комментарий:**

“The mind is a material sense and it cannot be born from an agent - the jiva called Sankarsana - because according to the Vedic philosophy this is seen as a contradiction."

## \|\| 2.2.43 \|\|

*vijnanadi-bhave va tad-apratisedhah \|\| 2.2.43 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the Supreme Lord’s form is made of knowledge, eternity and bliss, Pancaratra should not be rejected.**

**Комментарий:**

Here is the answer, so listen with an attentive mind, O dullard: All forms of the Supreme Lord starting with Vasudeva are explained as made of knowledge, eternity and bliss. Therefore, one should understand that words like 'birth' mean ‘appearance.’ Thus, you should not reject the Pancaratra doctrine.

## \|\| 2.2.44 \|\|

*vipratisedhac ca \|\| 2.2.44 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this regard, the idea that the jivas have birth is also denied. Thus, the idea that the expansions of the Supreme Lord have birth is by far defeated.**

## Section 3

## \|\| 2.3.1 \|\|

*na viyad asruteh \|\| 2.3.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Someone may object, “The sky is not produced, for the sruti does not say so.”**

**Комментарий:**

The followers of Nyaya and Vaisesika claim that the sky is not produced because its production is not mentioned in the sruti.

## \|\| 2.3.2 \|\|

*asti tu \|\| 2.3.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is not true, for there are indeed statements.**

**Комментарий:**

The followers of the Vedas say that there are statements in the sruti about the production of the sky.

## \|\| 2.3.3 \|\|

*gauny asambhavac chabdac ca \|\| 2.3.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Someone my object, “The meaning is secondary, for the sky cannot be produced, and because the sruti declares its eternity.**

**Комментарий:**

The logicians again claim that such a statement from the sruti is considered in a figurative sense, for the production of the sky is not possible, and because the sruti says that the sky is eternal (amrta).

## \|\| 2.3.4 \|\|

*syac caikasya brahma-sabdavat \|\| 2.3.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“One word can have a secondary meaning, just like the same word 'brahman’ is used both is its primary and secondary senses.”**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The word ‘sambhuta' in this passage of the Taittiriya Upanisad is equally applied to the five elements, but it should be understood as having different meanings, for the same is the case with the word ‘brahman. ’ In the case of the sky, the word ‘sambhuta ’ means appearance, and in the case of air and so on, it means production. ”

## \|\| 2.3.5 \|\|

*pratijna-hanir avyatirekac chabdebhyah \|\| 2.3.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is not so, because in this way the stated subject matter would be disregarded, because there is nothing besides the Supreme Lord, and because of other scriptural statements.**

**Комментарий:**

Here the followers of the Vedas reply: The meaning cannot be secondary because in this way the declaration of the sruti that ‘by knowing Him only everything is known' would be disregarded, because of the statement that before creation there was only one existent being, and because the sky is an effect, hence non-different from the Supreme Lord, the learned say that it must have been produced.

## \|\| 2.3.6 \|\|

*yavad-vikaram tu vibhago lokavat \|\| 2.3.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever transformation exists, that is part of the Supreme Lord, just as we see in the world.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as we see that transformations like pots are parts of earth, similarly whatever transformation exists, it is all part of the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.3.7 \|\|

*etena matarisva vyakhyatah \|\| 2.3.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Air is also explained by this statement.**

**Комментарий:**

By this statement regarding the origin of the sky, air is also described as originating from the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.3.8 \|\|

*asambhavas tu sato’nupapatteh \|\| 2.3.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The eternally existent Supreme Lord has no birth, for this is not possible.**

**Комментарий:**

The sky and the other elements are said to be born from the Supreme Lord, Whose form is eternally existent. Something eternally existent cannot be born, for its origin does not exist. Those who are intelligent accept that there is only one original cause of everything.

## \|\| 2.3.9 \|\|

*tejo’tas tatha hy aha \|\| 2.3.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Fire is produced from air, for the sruti declares so.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti declares that fire is produced from air.

## \|\| 2.3.10 \|\|

*apah \|\| 2.3.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Water is produced from fire, for the sruti declares so.**

**Комментарий:**

Water is produced from fire.

## \|\| 2.3.11 \|\|

*prthivi \|\| 2.3.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Earth is produced from water, for the fruti declares so.**

**Комментарий:**

From water, earth is produced.

## \|\| 2.3.12 \|\|

*adhikara-rupa-sabdantarebhyah \|\| 2.3.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because this is the topic, because earth’s colour is dark, and because there are other scriptural statements.**

**Комментарий:**

The word ‘anna’ is considered earth because this is the topic, because its colour is dark, and because there are other scriptural statements to this effect.

## \|\| 2.3.13 \|\|

*tad-abhidhyanad eva tu tal-lingat sah \|\| 2.3.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is the cause of all these elements, for there is evidence that this is accomplished by His mere will.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If the sky and so on are the cause of the subsequent elements, this would undermine the concept that the Supreme Lord is the cause, and thus the scriptural statements presented by you would be rejected.” It is not so, for there is evidence that on account of His will, the creation of the whole universe is done by the Supreme Lord in the form of sky and the rest. Therefore, His status as the cause is not undermined.

## \|\| 2.3.14 \|\|

*viparyayena tu kramo’ta upapadyate ca \|\| 2.3.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And the dissolution of these elements takes place in the reverse order.**

**Комментарий:**

The order of the dissolution of the sky and the other elements is exactly the opposite order in which their creation is described in the Upanisads.

## \|\| 2.3.15 \|\|

*antara vijnana-manasi kramena tal-lingad iti cen navisesat \|\| 2.3.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that this interpretation is not correct because the intelligence and the mind are not mentioned in this order as indicated in the scriptures, we say that this is not true, for they are included among similar substances.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In this way, the creation and dissolution of the elements in a progressive and regressive order is not possible, for it is seen in a scriptural sentence that the life-air is created before the sky and the rest. ” Please do not speak so, for by mentioning the creation of the sky and the rest, the creation of the life-air and all the subsequent senses is also implied. Since these are all equally classified as material elements, this is consistent with their respective order. Therefore, the best among the wise should not consider that their respective order of creation is meant in the sentence starting with ‘etasmat, ’ where a mere list of material substances is shown.

## \|\| 2.3.16 \|\|

*caracara-vyapasrayas tu syat tad-vyapadeso’bhaktas tad-bhava-bhavitvat \|\| 2.3.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Any word in connection with a moving or non-moving thing primarily denotes the Supreme Lord, for thus in the future one realizes that they are His expanded forms.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “It is not possible that the Supreme Lord takes the form of the sky and the rest, as previously stated, for ordinary words like 'sky' are respectively applied only to the sky and so on, and not to the Supreme Lord." It is not so, for the primary meaning of the words ‘sky,' etc., that denote moving and non-moving things, is applied to the Supreme Soul. This is the comprehension attained in the future by studying the scriptures.

## \|\| 2.3.17 \|\|

*natma sruter nityatvac ca tabhyah \|\| 2.3.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The individual soul is not produced, for its eternity is known from the sruti and the smrti.**

**Комментарий:**

Having thus described the Supreme Lord, the jiva's nature is now described. The jiva is not created like the sky and the other elements, for its eternity is known from both the sruti and the smrti.

## \|\| 2.3.18 \|\|

*jno’ta eva \|\| 2.3.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, the individual soul is a knower.**

**Комментарий:**

The jiva is neither considered to be mere consciousness nor a mere conscious being. The wise consider the jiva as consciousness itself and having consciousness as an attribute.

## \|\| 2.3.19 \|\|

*utkranti-gaty-agatinam \|\| 2.3.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The individual soul is atomic, for the scriptures describe its departure from the body and its moving in and out of the body.**

**Комментарий:**

By departing and moving in and out of the body, thejivas ’ atomic size is declared. If they were all-pervading, it would not be at all possible to move in and out.

## \|\| 2.3.20 \|\|

*svatmana cottarayoh \|\| 2.3.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The action of moving in and out of the body is connected with the soul itself.**

**Комментарий:**

The departure from the body may be possible by giving up bodily identification, but the action of moving in and out of the body is connected with the soul itself. Therefore, the jiva's all-pervasiveness is not substantiated.

## \|\| 2.3.21 \|\|

*nanur atac chruter iti cen netaradhikarat \|\| 2.3.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that the sruti states that the jiva is not atomic, we say that this is not true, for the topic is the Supreme Soul.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The jiva is not atomic, for its all-pervasiveness is accepted by the sruti. ” It is not so, for in such passages of the sruti the topic is the Supreme Soul.

## \|\| 2.3.22 \|\|

*sva-sabdonmanabhyam ca \|\| 2.3.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because in the sruti the jiva is expressed by the word 'atomic’ and compared to the extremity of a needle.**

**Комментарий:**

This is confirmed by the word expressing 'atomic size' (anu) and the comparison with the extremity of a needle.

## \|\| 2.3.23 \|\|

*avirodhas candanavat \|\| 2.3.23 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no contradiction, for the presence of consciousness is felt throughout the body, just like sandalwood paste.**

**Комментарий:**

Although the jiva is atomic, this does not contradict the fact that it experiences pleasure and pain throughout the body, for it is endowed with knowledge as its attribute. The wise know that by its consciousness, the soul is aware of the whole body, just as the coolness of a drop of sandalwood paste on the forehead is felt through the whole body.

## \|\| 2.3.24 \|\|

*avasthiti-vaisesyad iti cen nabhyupagamad dhrdi hi \|\| 2.3.24 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that sandalwood paste is particularly situated on a place, but the jiva is not, this is not true, for from the scriptures it is understood that it is situated in the heart.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “A drop of sandalwood paste situated on one place in the body is visible, but the soul is not. ” It is not so, for the sruti states that the soul is situated in the heart.

## \|\| 2.3.25 \|\|

*gunad valokavat \|\| 2.3.25 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Or because the quality of consciousness pervades the whole body, just like light.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as the sun and other luminous bodies spread far away through their quality of light, similarly the soul pervades the whole body through its quality of consciousness.

## \|\| 2.3.26 \|\|

*vyatireko gandhavat tatha hi darsayati \|\| 2.3.26 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The difference between the consciousness and the conscious is like that of the fragrance and the fragrant, for the scriptures teach so.**

**Комментарий:**

Just like fragrance goes far away from its receptacle, and so does something endowed with it, similarly the quality of consciousness spreads far, and so does the soul endowed with it.

## \|\| 2.3.27 \|\|

*prthag upadesat \|\| 2.3.27 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because the scriptures teach the difference.**

**Комментарий:**

In the sruti it is taught that consciousness is different from the jiva, therefore I consider that the jiva consists of consciousness and has consciousness as its attribute.

## \|\| 2.3.28 \|\|

*tad-guna-saratvat tu tad-vyapadesah prajnavat \|\| 2.3.28 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva is designated as consciousness itself because this quality is its very essence, just like the Supreme Lord is called ‘wise.’**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If the jiva possesses consciousness, then how can it have consciousness for its form? The sruti states that the jiva is only pure consciousness.” In answer to this, we say that because this quality is the unchangeable essence in the soul, it is its very form, just like the learned call the Supreme Lord as 'prajhavan' (wise), although He is knowledge personified.

## \|\| 2.3.29 \|\|

*yavad atma-bhavitvac ca na dosas tad-darsanat \|\| 2.3.29 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Consciousness exists as long as the soul exists. Therefore, there is no fault, for this is seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti shows that the existence of this quality is concomitant with the existence of the soul, just like fire and heat. Thus, the supposed fault is removed.

## \|\| 2.3.30 \|\|

*pumstvadivat tv asya sato’bhivyakti-yogat \|\| 2.3.30 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The soul’s consciousness exists even in deep sleep, like virility in ■ childhood, for later it becomes manifest in the waking state.**

**Комментарий:**

Consciousness is also existent in the state of deep sleep, for it becomes manifest in the waking state, just like virility exists in childhood and becomes manifest in youth.

## \|\| 2.3.31 \|\|

*nityopalabdhy-anupalabdhi-prasango’nyatara-niyamo vanyatha \|\| 2.3.31 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If the soul had no internal organ, then the result would be either eternal perception or eternal non-perception. Or else, perception and non-perception would be totally incidental.**

**Комментарий:**

The internal organ is the cause of the manifestation of knowledge. In accepting the opinion of those who do not accept the existence of the internal organ, when the soul's senses are in proximity with the sense objects, there should always be either perception or non-perception. Or if the power of the senses is removed from the soul, then the established order of things to accomplish anything would become totally incidental. Therefore, the learned must accept that the jiva has an internal organ.

## \|\| 2.3.32 \|\|

*karta sastrarthavattvat \|\| 2.3.32 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva is the doer, for then the instructions in the scriptures are meaningful.**

**Комментарий:**

The jiva is the doer, and not the intelligence and the senses. Otherwise, the scriptural injunctions would be useless.

## \|\| 2.3.33 \|\|

*upadanad viharopadesac ca \|\| 2.3.33 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the jiva accepts the senses, and also because the scriptures describe that it sports even after liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Because it accepts the senses as instruments, the doership of action must belong to thejiva. Also, because the scriptures describe that even in the liberated stage the jiva sports with women, and so on.

## \|\| 2.3.34 \|\|

*vyapadesac ca kriyayam na cen nirdesa-viparyayah \|\| 2.3.34 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva is the doer of action because it is stated so, otherwise the statement would be different.**

**Комментарий:**

The doership of the jiva in all activities is stated in the scriptures. If its doership is not substantiated, then the sruti which indicates it should have the instrumental case instead of the nominative case.

## \|\| 2.3.35 \|\|

*upalabdhivad aniyamah \|\| 2.3.35 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If the jiva were not the doer, then there would be no certainty regarding work and its fruits, as in the case of perception.**

**Комментарий:**

In a similar way, on account of the variety of actions, the jiva’s engagement in pious and impious activities would also be at random, as in the case of perception.

## \|\| 2.3.36 \|\|

*sakti-viparyayat \|\| 2.3.36 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Although a doer, the jiva is not independent, for the Lord and the jiva have opposite powers.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If both the Supreme Lord and the jiva are the doers, as accepted by you, then the jiva would never perform anything undesirable, just as the Lord does not. ” It is not so, for they have opposite powers. It is heard from the scriptures that the Supreme Lord is independent, and the jiva is dependent on Him and the effects of karma.

## \|\| 2.3.37 \|\|

*samadhy-abhavac ca \|\| 2.3.37 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the jiva is conditioned when it lacks perfection in yoga.**

**Комментарий:**

Even the jiva can be endowed with powers by becoming accomplished by practising the eight limbs of yoga. Lacking this, the jiva is a mundane doer and experiences hellish torments.

## \|\| 2.3.38 \|\|

*yatha ca taksobhayatha \|\| 2.3.38 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Both by its own will and by using its senses, the jiva is a doer like a carpenter.**

**Комментарий:**

When a carpenter wishes to engage in work with his axe to produce things with his hands, he is a doer, but when he does not wish, he is not a doer. In the same way, when the jiva endowed with senses like speech wishes to work, it is considered a doer, but not when it does not wish.

## \|\| 2.3.39 \|\|

*parat tu tac-chruteh \|\| 2.3.39 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva’s doership depends on the Supreme Lord, for this is stated in the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

It is heard from the sruti that the doership of the jiva is dependent on the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.3.40 \|\|

*krta-prayatnapeksas tu vihita-pratisiddhavaiyarthyadibhyah \|\| 2.3.40 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Supreme Lord engages the jivas in various activities, He takes into consideration their previous work, for in this way He is not at fault, and the injunctions and prohibitions in the scriptures are not purposeless.**

**Комментарий:**

Taking into consideration their previous work, the Supreme Lord engages the jivas in various activities, just like a cloud. Therefore, the Supreme Soul is not blamed with partiality and cruelty. Thus, the scriptural injunctions and so on are also not purposeless.

## \|\| 2.3.41 \|\|

*amso nana-vyapadesad anyatha capi dasa-kitavaditvam adhiyata eke \|\| 2.3.41 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to many descriptions in the scriptures, the soul is part of the Supreme Lord, Who is therefore also described in different ways, such as slave, gambler, and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

On account of the variety of relations mentioned in the scriptures, such as controlled and controller, the jiva is described as a part of the Supreme Lord. Since the Lord is also described as slave and so on, it is understood that this refers to the living entities, who are His parts and parcels.

## \|\| 2.3.42 \|\|

*mantra-varnat \|\| 2.3.42 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the description in the Vedic mantras.**

**Комментарий:**

From the description in the Vedic mantras it is clearly understood that the jiva is a part of the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.3.43 \|\|

*api smaryate \|\| 2.3.43 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The same is also stated by the smrtis.**

**Комментарий:**

In the Bhagavad-gita also the jiva is described as part of the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.3.44 \|\|

*prakasadivan naivam parah \|\| 2.3.44 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is not affected by the experience of the jivas, just like the sun is not affected by its reflections.**

**Комментарий:**

Although the jivas are non-different from the Supreme Lord, still their happiness and distress do not befall Him, just as a defect in the reflection does not affect the sun.

## \|\| 2.3.45 \|\|

*smaranti ca \|\| 2.3.45 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The smrtis also say so.**

**Комментарий:**

The smrtis also confirm that the jiva’s faults do not exist in the Supreme Soul, although they are related as part and whole.

## \|\| 2.3.46 \|\|

*anujna-pariharau deha-sambandhaj jyotir-adivat \|\| 2.3.46 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Permission and exclusion are done due to the souls’ connection with the body, just as in the case of fire and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Although all jivas are equally considered parts of the Supreme Lord, brahmanas and others recite the Vedas, but not sudras and other lower classes, for this depends on the connection of a soul with a pure or impure body. Fire from the house of a learned brahmana is to be taken, but fire from a cemetery is to be rejected.

## \|\| 2.3.47 \|\|

*asantates cavyatikarah \|\| 2.3.47 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because there is no connection between them, the jivas’ experiences do not intermingle.**

**Комментарий:**

Because the jivas are atomic, hence different in each body, their experience of pleasure and pain is not intermingled with each other's. This is the view of the Upanisads.

## \|\| 2.3.48 \|\|

*abhasa eva ca \|\| 2.3.48 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva merely appears like the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "It is sometimes heard that God and the jiva are both made by maya. ” This is true in the case of apparent, temporary forms that are different than their previously stated eternal forms, which are made of eternity, knowledge and bliss. Just like Krsna and Paundraka are both known as Vasudeva.

## \|\| 2.3.49 \|\|

*adrstaniyamat \|\| 2.3.49 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jivas cannot be all-pervading, for in this way their karma would intermingle.**

**Комментарий:**

In the opinion of the Samkhya philosophers, the multiple jivas are allpervading. If it were so, then the results of their karma would be irregular, and consequentially their experience of suffering and happiness would intermingle with each other's.

## \|\| 2.3.50 \|\|

*abhisandhyadisv api caivam \|\| 2.3.50 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The same is also true in the case of desires, hate, and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Desires, hate, and so on are said to be defined by the results of past karma, and if they were similarly connected simultaneously with all jivas, then the established order of things would be broken.

## \|\| 2.3.51 \|\|

*pradesad iti cen nantarbhavat \|\| 2.3.51 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that the order of things is due to the different bodies of the all-pervading souls, this is not true, for then they would also pervade everyone’s body.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The order of things is due to the differences of bodies. ”In this case also, since you think that the souls are all-pervading everyone would be inside everyone else’s body, and thus no established order would be possible.

## Section 4

## \|\| 2.4.1 \|\|

*tatha pranah \|\| 2.4.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The senses are similarly produced by the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous section of this chapter, the theories regarding the sky and so on, which contradict the sruti were refuted. In this section, the theories regarding the senses, etc., which contradict the sruti are refuted. Just as the elements like the sky and the rest are produced by the Supreme Lord, so are the senses like the eyes, which are named 'prana.'

## \|\| 2.4.2 \|\|

*gauny asambhavat \|\| 2.4.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their production is taken in a figurative sense, for literally it is not possible.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti passages that describe the production of the senses are figurative, for their production is not possible. This impossibility was shown by the great King in his commentary.

## \|\| 2.4.3 \|\|

*tat-prak srutes ca \|\| 2.4.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because it is heard from the sruti that the senses existed before creation.**

**Комментарий:**

It is also heard from the sruti that the senses existed before the creation of the sky and the rest.

## \|\| 2.4.4 \|\|

*tat-purvakatvad vacah \|\| 2.4.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because before the creation, only the Supreme Lord had a name.**

**Комментарий:**

It is impossible that there was anything named other than the Supreme Lord before creation, therefore the senses are considered to be born from Him, for they were born after creation.

## \|\| 2.4.5 \|\|

*sapta gater visesitatvac ca \|\| 2.4.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“From the sruti, the senses are understood to be seven, and also because of their specific functions.”**

**Комментарий:**

“The senses are seven in number, for this is understood from the sruti, and because of their different functions. ” This is the statement of those who are not learned.

## \|\| 2.4.6 \|\|

*hastadayas tu sthite’to naivam \|\| 2.4.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is not so, for in the sruti it is actually established that there are eleven senses, including the hands and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Because the sruti also mentions the hands, legs, and so on, the senses are considered to be eleven.

## \|\| 2.4.7 \|\|

*anavas ca \|\| 2.4.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The atomic sense objects are also born from the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

The atomic sense objects like sound and so on are also considered to be born from the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.4.8 \|\|

*sresthas ca \|\| 2.4.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The chief prana is also born from the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

The chief prana is also born from the Supreme Lord and is fivefold.

## \|\| 2.4.9 \|\|

*na vayu-kriye prthag upadesat \|\| 2.4.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The mukhya-prana is neither air nor a sensory function, for it is taught as being different from these.**

**Комментарий:**

The chief life-air is not considered to be the element of air nor a sensory function like speech, for in the sruti it is taught as being different from both.

## \|\| 2.4.10 \|\|

*caksur-adivat tu tat-saha sisty-adibhyah \|\| 2.4.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the eyes and the other senses, the chief prana is an instrument of the Jiva, for they are all taught together by the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

This chief prana is said to be dependent on the jiva, just like the eyes, for it is taught along with the senses as an instrument of the jiva.

## \|\| 2.4.11 \|\|

*akaranatvac ca na dosas tatha hi darsayati \|\| 2.4.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti teaches that there is no fault if the life-airs have no objects of perception, for they do not work as senses.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If it is accepted that the life-airs are similar to the senses, then they should also have objects of perception like colour. ” It is not so. Because the life-airs do not work as senses, they have no objects of perception. The sruti teaches that the effect of the life-airs is the maintenance of the body, and because they perform the foremost service, they are said to be assistants of the jiva.

## \|\| 2.4.12 \|\|

*panca-vrttir manovad vyapadisyate \|\| 2.4.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One and the same prana is designated as fivefold, just like the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

According to its different functions, one same life-air is designated as fivefold, just like the Yoga philosophers say that one mind has five different functions.

## \|\| 2.4.13 \|\|

*anus ca \|\| 2.4.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Time is also atomic.**

**Комментарий:**

Time is also atomic and is born from the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.4.14 \|\|

*jyotir-ady-adhisthanam tu tad-amananat \|\| 2.4.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The senses are the dwelling places of the fire-god and other demigods, as stated by the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The senses in the form of speech and so on shown in the previous sutras are the dwelling places of their controlling deities, such as the fire-god, for it is so stated in the scriptures.

## \|\| 2.4.15 \|\|

*pranavata sabdat \|\| 2.4.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sensual pleasure is connected with the jiva who holds the senses, as stated by the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Although the demigods are connected with the senses, they cannot experience the pleasure produced by them, for this is connected with the jiva who holds the senses. In the body of a demigod, they have much better enjoyment. By the will of the Supreme Lord, the demigods merely activate the senses. Otherwise, they would experience the enjoyment in the bodies of dogs and the like, which is very contemptible.

## \|\| 2.4.16 \|\|

*tasya ca nityatvat \|\| 2.4.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And enjoyment always belongs to a particular embodied jiva.**

**Комментарий:**

Therefore, the enjoyment within a particular body always belongs to that particular jiva.

## \|\| 2.4.17 \|\|

*ta indriyani tad-vyapadesad anyatra sresthat \|\| 2.4.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With the exception of the mukhya-prana, all the life-airs are senses, for they are so designated.**

**Комментарий:**

Although both the sense organs, such as speech, and the life-airs are called ‘indriya ’ (sense), according to the sruti they are considered so with the exception of the chief life-air.

## \|\| 2.4.18 \|\|

*bheda-sruteh \|\| 2.4.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their difference is heard from the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The difference between the sense organs and the chief life-air is also heard from the scriptures.

## \|\| 2.4.19 \|\|

*vailaksanyac ca \|\| 2.4.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because both have distinct characteristics.**

**Комментарий:**

Their different characteristics are seen in their function - the sense organs perceive sense objects, and the chief life-air maintains the body. Therefore, both are different.

## \|\| 2.4.20 \|\|

*samjna-murti-klptis tu trivrt kurvata upadesat \|\| 2.4.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The division of names and forms was done by the Supreme Lord, Who divided the elements into three, as taught in the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

The division of names and forms described in the sentence starting with 'hantaham’ is to be understood as having been done by the Supreme Lord, Who divided the elements into three, for this is the instruction there.

## \|\| 2.4.21 \|\|

*mamsadi-bhaumam yatha-sabdam itarayos ca \|\| 2.4.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the scriptures, one’s body is made of earth in the form of flesh and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Whatever is eaten by a person in the form of earth, water and fire is known as the following effects within the body: flesh and so on are made of earth; urine and so on are made of water; and bones, etc., are made of fire. All this is accomplished by the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 2.4.22 \|\|

*vaisesyat tu tad-vadas tad-vadah \|\| 2.4.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Substances are named according to the predominance of a particular element.**

**Комментарий:**

Although there is a mutual combination of the elements of earth, water, and fire, according to the predominance of a particular element, things are named as made of earth, water, and fire.

## Chapter 3

## Section 1

## \|\| 3.1.1 \|\|

*tad-antara-pratipattau ramhati samparisvaktah prasna-nirupanabhyam \|\| 3.1.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From the questions and answers, it is understood that when the jiva attains other planets, it goes accompanied by the subtle elements.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous chapter, the transcendental nature of the Supreme Lord was explained along with His energies. In this chapter, the means (sadhana) to attain Him, such as detachment, is shown. It is understood from the questions and answers in the Pancagni-vidya section that while attaining different planets, the jiva goes accompanied by the subtle elements.

## \|\| 3.1.2 \|\|

*try-atmakatvat tu bhuyastvat \|\| 3.1.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only water is mentioned there because of its predominance and because it is comprised of three elements.**

**Комментарий:**

Although therein only water is mentioned, still earth, water and fire are to be taken because water is a combination of all three of them. The learned consider that only water is mentioned because of its predominance.

## \|\| 3.1.3 \|\|

*prana-gates ca \|\| 3.1.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the departure of the chief life-air from the body, the departure of the other elements is also implied.**

**Комментарий:**

When the sruti says that the life-airs leave from a dead body, it means that the other elements also leave, for the life-airs cannot move without the elements, which are their resting-place.

## \|\| 3.1.4 \|\|

*agny-adi-gati-sruter iti cen na bhaktatvat \|\| 3.1.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that the fruti states that speech and so on enter into fire, etc., we say that this is not to be taken literally, for the meaning is figurative.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Then how is it that the sruti states that speech and so on enter into fire and the other elements?”. There is no fault there, for the meaning is figurative. By stating that they enter into fire and so on, the sruti means that the senses of speech and so on cease to assist the body.

## \|\| 3.1.5 \|\|

*prathame’sravanad iti cen na ta eva hy upapatteh \|\| 3.1.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that water is not mentioned in the first oblation, we say that it actually is, for water is the appropriate interpretation for the word ‘sraddha. '**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In the first oblation, the sruti does not mention the word ‘ap' (water) but rather the word ‘sraddha’ (faith). Therefore, how can the fifth oblation be water in the form of a human?” Please do not speak so, for the word ‘sraddha’ there is taken in the sense of water. Because water and faith are essential for performing Vedic rituals, such interpretation of this word is appropriate.

## \|\| 3.1.6 \|\|

*asrutatvad iti cen nestadi-karinam pratiteh \|\| 3.1.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that it is not mentioned that the jiva accompanies the elements, we say that this is not true, for it is understood that sacrificers attain heaven.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In those questions and answers, the sruti only speaks about the subtle elements leaving the body - there is no mention about the jiva going along with them. ” Please do not speak so, for therein is mentioned that those who perform Vedic sacrifices attain the moon planet for heavenly enjoyment.

## \|\| 3.1.7 \|\|

*bhaktam vanatmavittvat tatha hi darsayati \|\| 3.1.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The statement that those who attain heaven become food for the demigods is figurative, for it refers to those who have no spiritual knowledge, as the sruti shows.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “There is no enjoyment in heaven, for those who reach there are eaten by the demigods. ” It is not so. The statement that they are eaten is figurative, and it actually means that the demigods are served by them. The sruti says that this is the case of those who do not have spiritual knowledge, but not of those who are self-realized.

## \|\| 3.1.8 \|\|

*krtatyaye’nusayavan drsta-smrtibhyam yathetam anevam ca \|\| 3.1.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the iruti and the smrti, when its pious results are exhausted, the jiva, carrying its remaining karma, descends from the moon planet by partly crossing the same path and partly a different one.**

**Комментарий:**

It is understood from the sruti and the smrti that when the results of pious deeds are exhausted by enjoying there, the jiva descends from the moon planet accompanied by its remaining karma. The path by which the jiva went to the moon planet for enjoying is almost the same by which it descends because it partly goes by another path.

## \|\| 3.1.9 \|\|

*caranad iti cen nopalaksanartheti karsnajinih \|\| 3.1.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that it is only by one’s conduct (carana) and not by the remaining karma that one attains a particular birth, then Karsnajini says that actually 'carana’ means remaining karma.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue “It is heard from the sruti that there are two kinds of conduct (carana), good and bad, from which one attains a pious or impious birth, and not by the remaining karma, for such a view is faulty. ” In the opinion of Karsnajini, the word ‘carana’ in the sruti means ‘remaining karma,’ and therefore there is no fault.

## \|\| 3.1.10 \|\|

*anarthakyam iti cen na tad-apeksatvat \|\| 3.1.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that in this case, conduct would be useless, we say that this is not true, for it is also required.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If remaining karma is considered the cause to attain a particular birth, then conduct would be useless. ” It is not so, for conduct is also required. One who gives up good conduct is certainly never qualified to perform any Vedic ritual.

## \|\| 3.1.11 \|\|

*sukrta-duskrte eveti tu badarih \|\| 3.1.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Badari, these two words mean pious and sinful activities.**

**Комментарий:**

Badari says that these two words mean "pious activity" and "sinful activity. "

## \|\| 3.1.12 \|\|

*anistadi-karinam api ca srutam \|\| 3.1.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“It is also heard from the sruti that those who do not perform sacrifices also attain the moon.”**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "It is equally mentioned in the sruti that both those who perform sacrifices and those who do not perform will attain the moon planet."

## \|\| 3.1.13 \|\|

*samyamane tv anubhuyetaresam arohavarohau tad-gati-darsanat \|\| 3.1.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is seen in the scriptures that sinful people go to hell and descend into lower species after experiencing hellish torments.**

**Комментарий:**

It is not so. It is seen in the scriptures that those who are very sinful go to the abode of Yamaraja, and after experiencing hellish torments, they descend into the lower species of life.

## \|\| 3.1.14 \|\|

*smarantica \|\| 3.1.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sages also say so.**

**Комментарий:**

The sages also say so.

## \|\| 3.1.15 \|\|

*api ca sapta \|\| 3.1.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are seven main hellish planets.**

**Комментарий:**

There are seven main hellish planets that the sinful have to endure.

## \|\| 3.1.16 \|\|

*tatrapi ca tad-vyaparad avirodhah \|\| 3.1.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the hellish planets, Citragupta and others administer punishment under the order of Yamaraja. Therefore, there is no contradiction.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Citragupta and others are the punishers, and not Yamaraja. How can your statement that the punishment is administered by Yamaraja be consistent?" In the hellish planets, Citragupta and others administer punishment following the orders of Yamaraja, therefore there is no fault.

## \|\| 3.1.17 \|\|

*vidya-karmanor iti tu prakrtatvat \|\| 3.1.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is only by knowledge and ritualistic activities that one attains the moon, for this is the context of that passage of the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “After suffering in hell, the sinful also go to the moon, for in that passage the word ‘sarva’ (all) is used." In my opinion the sinful do not go to the moon because the context seen in that passage is knowledge by which one attains the path of the demigods, and ritualistic activities by which one attains the path of the forefathers. Thus, given the context, the learned take the meaning of the word 'air' in a restricted sense.

## \|\| 3.1.18 \|\|

*na trtiye tathopalabdheh \|\| 3.1.18  \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Others remain in the third place, for they cannot attain the moon.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In order to fulfil all the five oblations, everyone must go to the moon. ” It is not so, because the moon is not attained by everyone. There is a limitation in the number of oblations amongst the lower species such as flies, mosquitoes, and so on, that occupy the third place. Therefore, the five oblations described in the sruti are applicable in the case of those who go to the moon, and not of those who go down, for this is not mentioned. This is the conclusion.

## \|\| 3.1.19 \|\|

*smaryate’pi caloke \|\| 3.1.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sages say that some may obtain a human body in this world without the fifth oblation.**

**Комментарий:**

The sages say that personalities like Krpa and Drona take birth without the need of a womb.

## \|\| 3.1.20 \|\|

*darsanac ca \|\| 3.1.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is also seen in the case of lower species.**

**Комментарий:**

It is seen that living entities are born from sprouts and from moist heat without the fifth oblation.

## \|\| 3.1.21 \|\|

*trtiya-sabdavarodhah samsokajasya \|\| 3.1.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The living beings born from moist heat are included among those designated as being born from sprouts.**

**Комментарий:**

There are four kinds of living beings - those born from wombs, from eggs, from moist heat, and from sprouts. However, with the exception of those born from moist heat, only three were mentioned in the sruti. Therefore, the reasoning is shown herein. Those born from moist heat are considered to be included in the third class of beings designated ‘udbhijja’ (born from sprouts), for both have a similar birth, the difference being that the latter are born from earth, while the former from water.

## \|\| 3.1.22 \|\|

*tat-svabhavyapattir upapatteh \|\| 3.1.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Upon descending, the jiva takes up a form similar to ether and so on, for this is logical.**

**Комментарий:**

The mode of the aforementioned descent is now analysed. It is mentioned in the sruti that upon descending, the jiva takes up the form of ether and so on. The intended meaning is that the jiva takes up a form similar to ether and so on, for this is logical.

## \|\| 3.1.23 \|\|

*naticirena visesat \|\| 3.1.23 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The descent does not take long, for there is a specific mention.**

**Комментарий:**

Attaining a form similar to ether and so on, the jiva quickly reaches the earth. Because of the statement ‘abhyaso ha yat' in the sruti, it is understood that the jiva goes through this swiftly.

## \|\| 3.1.24 \|\|

*anyadhisthite purvavad abhilapat \|\| 3.1.24 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The plants which carry the descending jiva belong to other jivas, for such a connection has already been stated previously.**

**Комментарий:**

The statement that the jiva assumes a form like rice is also not to be taken literally. Those plants actually belong to other jivas, and the connection of the descending jiva with them is like the previously mentioned connection with ether and so on.

## \|\| 3.1.25 \|\|

*asuddham iti cen na sabdat \|\| 3.1.25 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that such a birth is the impure result of animal sacrifice, we say that this is not true, for the scriptures declare such sacrifices as pure.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Birth in the form of rice and so on is the result of sacrifices performed in a previous life, which involved violence. ” It is not so, for it is understood from the scriptures that such violence in Vedic sacrifices is not impure.

## \|\| 3. 1.26 \|\|

*retah-sig-yogo’tha \|\| 3. 1.26 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva then becomes united with a father who will emit semen.**

**Комментарий:**

From the state of rice, the jiva then goes into the body of a father who will emit semen.

## \|\| 3.1.27 \|\|

*yoneh sariram \|\| 3.1.27 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva is then injected into the womb, from where it attains another body.**

**Комментарий:**

The jiva combined with the semen is then injected into the womb and once again obtains a body full of multiple miseries.

## Section 2

## \|\| 3.2.1 \|\|

*sandhye srstir aha hi \|\| 3.2.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The creation of dreams is done by the jiva, for the sruti says so.”**

**Комментарий:**

Having spoken about detachment, Vyasadeva now shows the position of the jiva. Someone may argue, “The creation of chariots and so on in dreams is made by the jiva and is real, for the sruti says so. ”

## \|\| 3.2.2 \|\|

*nirmataram caike putradayas ca \|\| 3.2.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some Vedic followers say that the jiva is also the creator of desires in the form of sons, etc.**

**Комментарий:**

Followers of a certain Vedic branch say, “Therefore, the jiva is similarly the creator of desires for sons, etc. ”

## \|\| 3.2.3 \|\|

*maya-matram tu kartsnyenanabhivyakta-svarupatvat \|\| 3.2.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, dreams and desires are mere illusion, for by nature they do not become fully manifest as in the waking state.**

**Комментарий:**

It is not so. The creation of chariots and so in dreams is considered mere illusion. Because such a creation happens only in a very subtle vessel (nadi), it does not attain full manifestation as in the waking state.

## \|\| 3.2.4 \|\|

*sucakas ca hi sruter acaksate ca tad-vidah \|\| 3.2.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Although according to the scriptures dreams indicate things, those who know their essence say that they are false.**

**Комментарий:**

Although dreams may indicate auspicious and inauspicious things, still those who know their essence say that they are false.

## \|\| 3.2.5 \|\|

*parabhidhyanat tu tirohitam tato hy asya bandha-viparyayau \|\| 3.2.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By meditating on the Lord’s superior energy, one’s concealed spiritual power becomes manifest, for He is the cause of bondage and release for the jiva.**

**Комментарий:**

By meditating on the Lord's superior energy, one's concealed spiritual power becomes manifest, and therefore such an imaginary creation no more appears in the jiva's dream. However, by the influence of the Lord's maya, the creation in dreams appears, for it is mentioned in the scriptures that the living entities obtain bondage and liberation from the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 3.2.6 \|\|

*deha-yogad va so’pi \|\| 3.2.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The concealment of such a spiritual power also happens because of the connection with a material body.**

**Комментарий:**

It is said that the concealment of such a spiritual power also happens because of the connection with a material body.

## \|\| 3.2.7 \|\|

*tad-abhavo nadisu tac chruter atmani ca \|\| 3.2.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sruti, during deep sleep, the jiva goes into the middle portion of the heart and into the Supreme Soul via the nadis.**

**Комментарий:**

It is understood from the sruti that when the jiva has gone into deep sleep, it lies in the middle portion of the heart and into the Supreme Soul via the subtle vessels (nadis).

## \|\| 3.2.8 \|\|

*atah prabodho’smat \|\| 3.2.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, it is from the Supreme Soul that the jiva returns and becomes awake.**

**Комментарий:**

As it has been described that the Supreme Soul Himself is the place where the jiva rests in deep sleep, now it is stated that according to the sruti it is from the Supreme Soul that the jiva returns and becomes awake.

## \|\| 3.2.9 \|\|

*sa eva tu karmanusmrti-sabda-vidhibhyah \|\| 3.2.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the basis of its work and recollection, as well as the scriptural statements and injunctions, the same jiva who slept wakes up.**

**Комментарий:**

The jiva who wakes up is the same jiva who slept because the same work is done in the waking state, one remembers what was previously experienced, the scriptures say so, and because of the injunctions regarding work.

## \|\| 3.2.10 \|\|

*mugdhe’rdha-sampattih parisesat \|\| 3.2.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one faints, one attains the Supreme Soul only partially, for such a state is supplemental to the other three states.**

**Комментарий:**

When a person faints, that is understood as a state of half deep sleep. It is concluded that such a state is different from the other three states - waking, sleep, and deep sleep - for it is supplemental to them. These four states of the jiva are mentioned in the scriptures. The conclusion is that the jiva is different from these states and is the meaning of the word ‘tvam.’

## \|\| 3.2.11 \|\|

*na sthanato’pi parasyobhaya-lingam sarvatra hi \|\| 3.2.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no doubt that although He is situated in many places, the Supreme Lord is only one, for being present everywhere, He is characterized as one and many at the same time.**

**Комментарий:**

The sage Vyasadeva says that the Supreme Lord is referred to by the word 'tat. ’ There is no doubt that Lord Visnu is only one, and yet He is situated in many places. Because He is indeed everywhere, although one, He manifests Himself as many.

## \|\| 3.2.12 \|\|

*na bhedad iti cen na pratyekam atad-vacanat \|\| 3.2.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that it is not so, for there is difference between the Lord’s forms, we say that this is not true, for the scriptures declare that each of them is non-different.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The Lord must be different in each form instead of one, for there is difference between mantras, worshippers, and so on. " It is not so, for it is seen that the scriptures state that there is no difference in the Lord's nature (svarupa) in all of His forms.

## \|\| 3.2.13 \|\|

*api caivam eke \|\| 3.2.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And there are others who say that the Supreme Lord just appears to be many.**

**Комментарий:**

Some say the one and the same Supreme Lord appears to be many.

## \|\| 3.2.14 \|\|

*arupavad eva hi tat-pradhanatvat \|\| 3.2.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is said to be formless because His very self is His form, which is superior to anything material.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord is said to be formless because His form is devoid of any material element. Being made of knowledge and bliss, His form is inconceivable and transcendental.

## \|\| 3.2.15 \|\|

*prakasavac cavaivasesyat \|\| 3.2.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His form is just like that of the sun light, for there is no difference between the Lord and His form or the sun and its light.**

**Комментарий:**

That transcendental form can be apprehended by devotion, which is self-luminous, just like the sun, which is made of light, is apprehended by the eyes, which are the sun's own manifestation.

## \|\| 3.2.16 \|\|

*aha ca tan-matram \|\| 3.2.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At some places, the Sruti says that His form is devoid of attributes.**

**Комментарий:**

At some places, the sruti states that the Supreme Lord also has one form devoid of attributes.

## \|\| 3.2.17 \|\|

*darsayati catho api smaryate \|\| 3.2.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Both the sruti and the smrti teach that the Supreme Lord is saguna and nirguna.**

**Комментарий:**

Both the sruti and the smrti clearly teach that one and the same Supreme Lord is endowed with transcendental attributes and devoid of material attributes.

## \|\| 3.2.18 \|\|

*ata evacopama suryakadivat \|\| 3.2.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because of His multiple manifestations, the Supreme Lord is therefore compared to the sun and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Because one and the same Supreme Lord appears in many forms among His devotees, in the scriptures He is compared with the sun and so on.

## \|\| 3.2.19 \|\|

*ambuvad agrahanat tu na tathatvam \|\| 3.2.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, the forms of the Supreme Lord are not like a reflection, for they cannot be perceived in the same way as a reflection is seen on water.**

**Комментарий:**

The idea in the given example that the Lord's form is conditional is rejected here. The form of the Supreme Lord is not unreal like that of a face seen reflected on water. All His forms are real, for they are His direct parts. Therefore, the said comparison is partially true and thus not faulty.

## \|\| 3.2.20 \|\|

*vrddhi-hrasa-bhaktvam antarbhavad ubhaya-samanjasyad evam \|\| 3.2.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Diminution and growth of the Lord’s form in His pastimes are possible, for this being part of His transcendental nature, both statements are correct.**

**Комментарий:**

Now in this sutra the peculiarity of the Lord's form in growing in His youth, diminishing in size in His childhood, and so on, is considered eternal, for this is part of the nature of His supreme inconceivable potency. Thus, both statements regarding His form are correct.

## \|\| 3.2.21 \|\|

*darsanac ca \|\| 3.2.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because this is seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

In this way, contradictory attributes seen in the Supreme Lord are considered to be possible by His inconceivable potency, as in pastimes like His being bound with a rope by Mother Yasoda, and so on.

## \|\| 3.2.22 \|\|

*prakrtaitavattvam hi pratisedhati tato braviti ca bhuyah \|\| 3.2.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti denies that the Supreme Lord has a form and then again speaks about His form.**

**Комментарий:**

The words of the Vedas deny that the Supreme Lord has a form but then again speak about His form. Therefore, the learned consider that His form is both saguna and nirguna.

## \|\| 3.2.23 \|\|

*tad avyaktam aha hi \|\| 3.2.23 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti says that the Supreme Lord is unmanifest.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The Supreme Lord is said to be devoid of material form. The concept that He is saguna and nirguna is not consistent with statements that He is formless. ” It is not so. At some places, the sruti says that the Supreme Lord is unmanifest. This means that the very same Supreme Lord is also present everywhere in His form devoid of attributes.

## \|\| 3.2.24 \|\|

*api samradhane pratyaksanumanabhyam \|\| 3.2.24 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is known from the fruti and the smrti that when the Supreme Lord is worshipped, He becomes manifest.**

**Комментарий:**

The means to know the Supreme Lord is now revealed - although He is unmanifest, upon being worshipped by the devotees, He becomes manifest. This is known from the sruti and the smrti, hence He has attributes. Since the Supreme Lord, Who is distinguished by His transcendental qualities (saguna), is non-different from His formless aspect (nirguna), knowledge about the unmanifest also becomes possible.

## \|\| 3.2.25 \|\|

*prakasavac cavaisesyam prakasas ca karmany abhyasat \|\| 3.2.25 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is non-different from His form, just like the sun and its light, for He continuously becomes manifest in His pastimes.**

**Комментарий:**

The objection on account of place and dimension has been refuted. Now the objection regarding the activities performed by the Lord in His pastimes is being refuted here. There is also no difference between the Lord and His form, for He continuously manifests Himself in His pastimes. He is the same, just like the sun and its light, as stated before. Thus, on account of His eternal and unlimited manifestations, the Supreme Soul, Who is the performer of inconceivable deeds, is eternally distinguished by His transcendental pastimes.

## \|\| 3.2.26 \|\|

*ato’nantena tatha hi lingam \|\| 3.2.26 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Unlimited forms are manifested by the unlimited Supreme Lord, for indications are seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Although there are unlimited devotees and their desires are similarly unlimited, still there is no deficiency in the unlimited Supreme Lord. He is already manifest according to their devotion and the form worshipped by them. This is understood from the indications in the scriptures.

## \|\| 3.2.27 \|\|

*ubhaya-vyapadesat tv ahi-kundalavat \|\| 3.2.27  \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because the scriptures declare so, the Supreme Lord is both saguna and nirguna, like a serpent and its coils.**

**Комментарий:**

Just like a serpent and its coils, the one and the same Supreme Lord is considered both saguna and nirguna,for both are stated in the scriptures. Although there is oneness between a serpent and its coils, still the concept of their difference is clear. Vedic scholars should conclude this concept of difference in the non-different Supreme Lord in a similar way.

## \|\| 3.2.28 \|\|

*prakasasrayavad va tejastvat \|\| 3.2.28  \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just as between the sun and its light there is oneness in the form of heat**

**Комментарий:**

Just as between the sun and its light there is oneness in the form of heat, similarly between the saguna and nirguna forms there is oneness in consciousness.

## \|\| 3.2.29 \|\|

*purvavad va \|\| 3.2.29 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just as in the previous comparison, there is no difference of manifestation.**

**Комментарий:**

Let the wise accept the previous comparison between a serpent and its coils here also. According to Vedic logic, difference is not expected to exist among individuals belonging to a single category.

## \|\| 3.2.30 \|\|

*pratisedhac ca \|\| 3.2.30 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because difference is denied in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

There is no difference between the attributes and their possessor, for such a difference is denied in the scriptures. It is by the power of 'visesa ’ that the appearance of difference is not prevented.

## \|\| 3.2.31 \|\|

*param atah setunmana-sambandha-bheda-vyapadesebhyah \|\| 3.2.31 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“There must be something besides the Supreme Lord, for this is understood from the scriptures by terms like limit, dimension, relation and difference.”**

**Комментарий:**

It has been previously ascertained that everything that exists is a form of the Supreme Lord. In this regard, someone may argue, “There is something else besides the Supreme Lord, for terms like limit, measure, relation and difference are mentioned in the scriptures. ” It is not so. Since nothing else exists, these terms cannot apply to anything else besides the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 3.2.32 \|\|

*samanyat tu \|\| 3.2.32 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is not so, for the word 'setu' (limit) is used only because of its similarity.**

**Комментарий:**

The word ‘setu’ is used because of the similarity with the concept of one who sets the limits. By this it is not meant that there is something beyond the Supreme Lord, for the scriptures stated that He assumes the form of everything that exists.

## \|\| 3.2.33 \|\|

*buddhy-arthah padavat \|\| 3.2.33 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Words like ‘quarter’ are meant for easy understanding.**

**Комментарий:**

Statements about the measure of the Supreme Lord are understood by the learned as meant for meditation, just like statements that fire, air, the sun, and the directions represent each one quarter of the adhidaivata form of the Supreme Lord (akasa). Such scriptural terms are employed for the purpose of easy understanding. No difference is implied by them, for the meditation taught there is on the non-differentiated aspect of the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 3.2.34 \|\|

*sthana-visesat prakasadivat \|\| 3.2.34 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Relation and difference are understood just like the light of the sun and the moon in different places.**

**Комментарий:**

Relation and difference mentioned in connection with the Supreme Lord are just like the relation of the sun and the moon with different places. Since all material limitations are false, what could make His oneness hindered?

## \|\| 3.2.35 \|\|

*upapattes ca \|\| 3.2.35 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the fruti demonstrates this.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti demonstrates the oneness of the Supreme Lord by statements like ‘svam apitah \* (when one is in deep sleep, one has entered into the Supreme Soul).

## \|\| 3.2.36 \|\|

*tathanya-pratisedhat \|\| 3.2.36 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And also because the scriptures deny the existence of anything else besides the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

This is also similarly understood from the scriptures where the existence of anything else besides the Supreme Lord is denied.

## \|\| 3.2.37 \|\|

*anena sarva-gatatvam ayama-sabdadibhyah \|\| 3.2.37 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the above refutation and from words that express His allpervasiveness, it is concluded that the Supreme Lord is omnipresent.**

**Комментарий:**

By refuting the misconception regarding terms such as ‘setu, ’ the concept that the Supreme Lord is everything has been confirmed. From words that express His all-pervasiveness, it is concluded that the Supreme Lord is indeed everything.

## \|\| 3.2.38 \|\|

*phalam ata upapatteh \|\| 3.2.38 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord gives the fruits of one’s deeds, for this is appropriate.**

**Комментарий:**

The living entities in various conditions of life attain the fruits of their deeds in the form of attaining happiness and decreasing distress as given by the Supreme Lord, for this is appropriate.

## \|\| 3.2.39 \|\|

*srutatvac ca \|\| 3.2.39 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the sruti says so.**

**Комментарий:**

And because this is also known from the sruti.

## \|\| 3.2.40 \|\|

*dharmam jaiminir ata eva \|\| 3.2.40 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaimini says that dharma gives the fruits of deeds.**

**Комментарий:**

On the basis of logic and the sruti, Jaimini says that dharma gives the fruits of one's deeds.

## \|\| 3.2.41 \|\|

*purvam tu badarayano hetu-vyapadesat \|\| 3.2.41 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vyasadeva says that the Supreme Lord gives the fruits, for He is their cause, as previously described.**

**Комментарий:**

Vyasadeva says that the Supreme Lord is the giver of the fruits, for He, the Supreme Conscious Being, is the one mentioned as the cause of the fruits, as previously described. It is seen that conscious living entities give fruits, and not unconscious things like pots.

## Section 3

## \|\| 3.3.1 \|\|

*sarva-vedanta-pratyayam codanady-avisesat \|\| 3.3.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only one concept is known in all Upanisads, for the injunctions and so on found in the Vedas are also non-different.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous part of this chapter, the Supreme Lord's form as eternity, knowledge and bliss personified has been proved. Now in this section, His attributes are under discussion. The one and the same Supreme Lord is to be known in all Upanisads, which divide His qualities. No difference in the injunctions and so on is seen anywhere in the Vedas, just as only one injunction for performing the Agnistoma sacrifice is seen, although there are different Vedic branches.

## \|\| 3.3.2 \|\|

*bhedan neti cen naikasyam api \|\| 3.3.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone argues that the object of knowledge cannot be the same because differences are mentioned, we say that this is not true, for difference is seen even within one and the same Vedic branch.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Since different attributes are described, the object of knowledge cannot be only one. ” Please do not have such a doubt, for different attributes are seen even in one Vedic branch.

## \|\| 3.3.3 \|\|

*svadhyayasya tathatvena samacare’dhikarac ca savavac ca tan-niyamah \|\| 3.3.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The rule is like the Sava sacrifice because the right for the traditional performance of sacrifices is determined by one’s respective branch of Vedic study.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If the performance of sacrifices like the Agnistoma described in different Vedic branches is taken as one, then merit would be acquired from them by performing all of them together, which is not desired. ” This is what we say: The Vedas teach the followers of different branches about sacrifices like the Agnistoma, in both different and non-different ways. This is the rule to be applied, otherwise there would be fault in the statements of the $ruti,for the right to perform sacrifices is restricted to one's respective branch. Just like the traditional performance of the Agnistoma is done only by the followers of the Atharva Veda branch, and not by others.

## \|\| 3.3.4 \|\|

*darsayati ca \|\| 3.3.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti also teaches this.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti also directly teaches everywhere that the Supreme Lord is the only object of knowledge.

## \|\| 3.3.5 \|\|

*upasamharo’rthabhedad vidhi-sesavat samane ca \|\| 3.3.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because there is no difference in the object, the combination of the Lord’s qualities is like the combination of subordinate injunctions when there is similarity.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The previous statement that the Supreme Lord is everything and everywhere is not true, for the combination of His qualities would not be possible according to this principle. ” It is not so. The mutual combination of the Supreme Soul's qualities is possible, for it is mentioned that there is no difference in His own nature among multiple forms which are everywhere. However, when there are contrary moods, then the qualities should not be combined into one. The learned whose thoughts are fixed on the Supreme Lord say that this is like in the performance of Agnihotras and so on.

## \|\| 3.3.6 \|\|

*anyathatvam sabdad iti cen navisesat \|\| 3.3.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that from the sruti it is understood that the combination of the Lord’s qualities is not possible, we say that this is not true, for the scriptural statements present similar evidence to their combination.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “From the sruti it is known that the Supreme Soul is a single object of devotion. Because this restricts the possibility of combination, His qualities should not be combined. ” It is not so, for all such scriptural statements present similar evidence to their combination.

## \|\| 3.3.7 \|\|

*na va prakarana-bhedat parovarivastvadivat \|\| 3.3.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A combination of qualities is not done when they are mentioned in different sections. Bhakti is best when performed by devotees, just as sacrificial rituals are best when performed by a consecrated person and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

The scriptures also have different sections pertaining to bhakti (devotion) and jnana (knowledge). Therefore, the wise consider the scriptural statements as being arranged into two kinds of sentences. In the section on jnana, the Supreme Lord appears like a portion of sea water. In the section on bhakti, He is compared with the sweet water of the Ganga. Having such transcendental attributes, the Supreme Soul is the only object of devotion in both sections, for He is always manifest as the reservoir of eternal and inconceivable forms. Just like sacrificial rituals are best when performed by a person consecrated according to the statements of the scriptural section on consecration, but not when performed by a non-consecrated one, similarly the activities described in the section on bhakti are best when performed by devotees.

## \|\| 3.3.8 \|\|

*samjnatas cet tad uktam asti tu tad api \|\| 3.3.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that by having the same designation as worshippers of the Supreme Lord, they should combine His qualities, we say that this is not true, for this has already been answered and there is an example.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Both those who engage in devotional service and those who cultivate spiritual knowledge are equally known as worshippers of the Supreme Lord, and therefore both should combine His qualities. ” It is not so. The solution to this objection has already been stated in the previous sutra. The combination of qualities would not be correctly done in this way. Just as it is seen that there are different kinds of meditations such as the Parovarivastva and so on, that are equally called Udgitha, despite their differences. Even though equally named worshippers of the Supreme Lord, one group does not combine the qualities described in the other, for there are differences in the meditation.

## \|\| 3.3.9 \|\|

*vyaptes ca samanjasam \|\| 3.3.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the Supreme Lord is all-pervading, meditation on His different features in childhood, youth, and so on, is appropriate.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord's body is made of eternity, knowledge and bliss, and because it is always all-pervading, His form worshipped by His devotees is manifest in many features, such as childhood, youth, and so on.

## \|\| 3.3.10 \|\|

*sarvabhedad anyatreme \|\| 3.3.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since the Lord’s associates are also non-different, they can be simultaneously present elsewhere.**

**Комментарий:**

It is concluded that since the Supreme Lord's associates, etc., in His pastimes are also non-different, their relation with Him exists in all pastimes. As for instance, Lord Visnu's magnanimous associates, manifesting themselves into double forms, were simultaneously present both in the house of Srutadeva as well as in the house of Bahulasva.

## \|\| 3.3.11 \|\|

*anandadayah pradhanasya \|\| 3.3.11  \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord has transcendental qualities such as bliss.**

**Комментарий:**

His associates derive happiness from Lord Hari’s happiness, as it is heard from the scriptures. Therefore, without Him, their happiness would not be possible.

## \|\| 3.3.12 \|\|

*priya-sirastvady-apraptir upacayapacayau hi bhede \|\| 3.3.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Qualities like ‘having joy as His head’ are not to be combined, for increase and decrease are possible only if there is difference.”**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Qualities such as 'having joy as His head’ are not to be combined with forms like Krsna, for wings, tail and the like do not befit Them. ” It is not so. There is no difference. Because forms like Krsna are being depicted here as bliss personified, these qualities are also to be combined with Them.

## \|\| 3.3.13 \|\|

*itare tv artha-samanyat \|\| 3.3.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Others combine these qualities because they equally mean bliss.**

**Комментарий:**

Others, the devotees in santa-rasa, combine qualities like wings, tail and the like as singular forms of bliss.

## \|\| 3.3.14 \|\|

*adhyanaya prayojanabhavat \|\| 3.3.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such qualities are only meant for meditation, since there is no other purpose. '**

**Комментарий:**

Those who are exclusively devoted to one form accept qualities which are proper for meditation on the Supreme Lord, and not others. The reason is that their meditation becomes successful by accepting only these qualities, while there would be no purpose by accepting other qualities.

## \|\| 3.3.15 \|\|

*atma-sabdac ca \|\| 3.3.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the word 'atma ’ is used in that passage.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti teaches that among joy and the other kinds of happiness, bliss is the ‘atma. ’ Therefore, those who are exclusively devoted to one form of the Supreme Lord consider this as a meditation on His blissfulness.

## \|\| 3.3.16 \|\|

*atma-grhitir itaravad uttarat \|\| 3.3.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The word 'atma' is understood as the Supreme Soul, just as in other scriptural passages He is always the one described as bliss personified.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “This passage refers to the jiva, for this is understood by the word ‘atma’ in the sentence ‘sarira atma.’ It is not so, for the Supreme Lord is always the one described as bliss personified, and no one else.

## \|\| 3.3.17 \|\|

*anvayad iti cet syad avadharanat \|\| 3.3.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that because the Supreme Lord is naturally connected with everything, the word ‘atma’ means the Jiva, we say that this is not true, for the word ‘atma ’ is used in a restricted sense as the Supreme Soul.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Being all-pervading the Supreme Soul is connected with everything everywhere, therefore the material body belongs primarily to the jiva, and not to Him. ” Please do not speak so. The sentence ‘esa eva’ (He only) denies the idea that the topic is anything else besides the Supreme Soul. Since He is described as the soul of bliss itself, the wise should understand that the Supreme Lord, the soul of all, Whose form is present within everyone's body, is the one described here.

## \|\| 3.3.18 \|\|

*karyakhyanad apurvam \|\| 3.3.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since He is described as the one Who produces everything, the Supreme Lord is unparalleled.**

**Комментарий:**

In the Brhad-aranyaka Upanisad (1.4.8), the 'atma' mentioned as 'dear' is considered to be the Supreme Lord, for He is described as the one involved in producing everything that exists. By the word 'isvara’ (Supreme Lord) used there, as well as by being the 'nearest companion of all,' the dearmost Supreme Lord is expressed as 'atma,' and no one else.

## \|\| 3.3.19 \|\|

*samana evam cabhedat \|\| 3.3.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Although the Supreme Lord’s form has a dimension, it is equally spiritual, for there is no difference in nature.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The Supreme Lord's form is seen as endowed with different limbs. Therefore, not being homogeneous, He cannot be a single soul. ” It is not so. Even though His form has variety and dimension, there is no difference in them. Just like any part of a golden statue is golden, similarly the Lord’s form is completely spiritual.

## \|\| 3.3.20 \|\|

*sambandhad evam anyatrapi \|\| 3.3.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because of their connection with the Supreme Lord, the qualities of the empowered jivas should be similarly combined.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as the attributes of the Lord’s many incarnations are mutually combined, the same is the case with the qualities of empowered (sakty-avesa) jivas like Prthu and others due to their connection with the Supreme Soul, like that of fire with iron.

## \|\| 3.3.21 \|\|

*navavisesat \|\| 3.3.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Or their qualities may not be combined, since they are similar to the other jivas.**

**Комментарий:**

Some scholars do not combine the qualities of those virtuous souls because they are similar to the other jivas in nature and attributes.

## \|\| 3.3.22 \|\|

*darsayati ca \|\| 3.3.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti also shows this.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti shows Devarsi Narada's inquisitiveness, although he is also an empowered jiva.

## \|\| 3.3.23 \|\|

*sambhrti-dyu-vyapty api catah \|\| 3.3.23 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some also meditate on the empowered jivas as having qualities like ‘being the supporter’ and ‘pervading the sky.’ Others do not because of the above reason.**

**Комментарий:**

Some meditate on the empowered jivas as having qualities such as ‘being the supporter' and ‘pervading the sky.' Others think that the jivas cannot have the same qualities as the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 3.3.24 \|\|

*purusa-vidyayam api cetaresam anamnanat \|\| 3.3.24 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The qualities described in the Purusa-sukta may also apply to the jiva. ” No, for there is no statement that they belong to others.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The sruti says that the Supreme Lord has qualities like having thousands of heads. Would such qualities not belong also to the jivas in the stages of anna-maya and so on, since they are also called 'purusa'?” No, because there is no such a statement in the scriptures regarding the conditioned souls, who are subject to undergo the transformations of material nature. Therefore, since the attributes of the conscious living entities are subject to transformation, they are not to be combined.

## \|\| 3.3.25 \|\|

*vedhady artha-bhedat \|\| 3.3.25 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Disturbances and the like happen if there is no connection with the Lord, the object of meditation.**

**Комментарий:**

Now it is stated that one whose mind is fixed on the Lord has no fault. When the demons disturbed the recitation of the Sama hymns performed by the demigods' speech and other senses, it is understood that it so happened because they were not in connection with the Supreme Lord. When the Supreme Lord is present in the mouth, then disturbances and the like are not possible due to the connection with Him.

## \|\| 3.3.26 \|\|

*hanau tupayana-sabda-sesatvat kusacchandah-stuty-upaganavat taduktam \|\| 3.3.26 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the aversion towards the Lord is lost, the jiva is said to be distinguished for attaining qualities similar to His. This is said to be like the singing of metrical hymns in praise of the kusas.**

**Комментарий:**

When its connection with the Supreme Lord is lost, the jiva's knowledge, bliss and so on disappear. When its aversion towards the Lord is lost, the jiva is said to attain similarity with the qualities of the Lord, but not completely. This is the difference. Just like by pronouncing the syllable ‘bha’ in the recitation of hymns in praise of the kusas this does not equate it with the syllables 'bha' of the Rg Veda. In the same way, thejiva attains qualities similar to those of the Supreme Lord, but not fully.

## \|\| 3.3.27 \|\|

*samparaye tartavyabhavat tatha hy anye \|\| 3.3.27 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those situated in bhakti are superior, for they have no sins as others like the jnanis.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the superiority of the path of bhakti over the path of jnana is stated. The word ‘samparaya ' means bhakti, and 'tartavavya ' means sin. Because the devotees' sins have already been destroyed, bhakti is considered distinguished. Other than the devotees, those who are jnanis overcome sin by knowledge. This is the purport of the sruti, otherwise the other statements would be purposeless, for bhakti becomes manifest when sins have already been destroyed.

## \|\| 3.3.28 \|\|

*chandata ubhayavirodhat \|\| 3.3.28 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When devotees face sinful reactions, it is by the will of the Lord, for there is no contradiction between both causes.**

**Комментарий:**

In cases like Bharata, whose sinful reaction produced the body of a deer, the cause is the Supreme Lord's will, which is dependent on the devotees ’ will. This is the excellence of bhakti, and the result increases joy, for both kinds of statements are dependent on the same principle.

## \|\| 3.3.29 \|\|

*gater arthavattvam ubhayathanyatha hi virodhah \|\| 3.3.29 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The paths of bhakti and jnana include all other paths and both lead to liberation, for otherwise the sruti would be contradicted.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “Several kinds of paths are known from the statements of the sruti and the smrti, so how can jnana and bhakti be considered the only two?” It is not so. Despite manifold paths, the goal is considered to be twofold. The conclusion is that these two paths include all the others, for otherwise the sruti would be contradicted when it says that liberation cannot be attained by performing one's duty, by begetting progeny, by wealth, and so on.

## \|\| 3.3.30 \|\|

*upapannas tal-laksanarthopalabdher lokavat \|\| 3.3.30 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One endowed with love is superior, for by love it is possible to conquer the Supreme Lord, just as a dedicated wife conquers her husband.**

**Комментарий:**
