Although it is heard from the scriptures that the fruits of bhakti are liberation and love, still the latter is the foremost fruit, for by love one can attain the goal of life, the Supreme Lord Himself, while by the former it is not possible to see Him. It is seen in this world that a woman who is dependent on her husband enjoys the utmost happiness, otherwise, she suffers, even if she has riches, grains, and so on.

## \|\| 3.3.31 \|\|

*aniyamah sarvasam avirodhah sabdanumanabhyam \|\| 3.3.31 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sruti and the smrti, there is no rule that all limbs of bhakti must be practised. There is no contradiction here.**

**Комментарий:**

There is no rule that bhakti produces fruits only when all its limbs combined are practised, such as remembering the Lord, and so on. It is seen that each one amongst all the limbs of bhakti gives liberation. There is no contradiction here, for evidence is clearly seen in the sruti and the smrti.

## \|\| 3.3.32 \|\|

*yavad-adhikaram avasthitir adhikarikanam \|\| 3.3.32 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Devotees in a position of authority remain in the universe so long as they have a duty.**

**Комментарий:**

There is also no such rule that all those who have devotion for Lord Hari attain liberation immediately. According to the scriptures, devotees like Lord Brahma and others, who hold positions of authority, remain in the universe so long as they have a duty, after which, by the desire of the Lord, they will attain liberation.

## \|\| 3.3.33 \|\|

*aksara-dhiyam tv avarodhah samanya-tad-bhavabhyam aupasadavat tad uktam \|\| 3.3.33 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“All the limbs expressed by the word 'aksara' should be combined, for they convey the same spiritual nature.” This is not true, for those conversant with the Vedas say that this is like the selection of a priest in the Upasad sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "All the limbs of jnana and bhakti are known by words that express the imperishable Brahman (aksara), and the wise count them among the means for attaining liberation. Devotion for Lord Krsna is the same as realizing one's identity with the impersonal Brahman, therefore there cannot be two different goals in a single path. " This is what we say: It is just like when in the performance of the Upasad sacrifice, the sacrificer chooses a priest to touch the sacrificial fire, and then others to do secondary functions. The first one who is selected is fortunate, and not the others. In the same way, the Supreme Lord gives love for God to a particular soul, and similarity with Himself to others. This is the statement of those conversant with the Vedas.

## \|\| 3.3.34 \|\|

*iyad amananat \|\| 3.3.34 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The happiness within the impersonal Brahman is limited, for it is so described.**

**Комментарий:**

A certain distinction between the Supreme Lord and the impersonal Brahman is stated here. The happiness within the impersonal Brahman is considered as mere consciousness, for it is described in the sentences of the Ananda-valli as being limited.

## \|\| 3.3.35 \|\|

*antara bhuta-gramavat svatmanah \|\| 3.3.35 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For the Lord’s devotee, to be within the impersonal Brahman is an impediment similar to this material world.**

**Комментарий:**

Therefore, for the Lord's devotee, the concept that oneself is the impersonal Brahman is said to be similar to being inside the material world, and it is considered an impediment according to the Srimad-Bhagavatam and other scriptures.

## \|\| 3.3.36 \|\|

*anyathabhedanupapattir iti cen nopadesantaravat \|\| 3.3.36 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that it is not so, otherwise instructions about nondifference would not be possible, we say that this is not true. Just as in the case of other instructions, they serve a particular purpose.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “You have to admit that realization of one's identity with the impersonal Brahman is superior. Otherwise, instructions on non-dualism given to Uddhava and others would not be possible.” It is not so. Just as the Supreme Lord Krsna teaches yoga and other such topics, similarly such instructions about non-difference are meant to remove the feeling of separation.

## \|\| 3.3.37 \|\|

*vyatiharo visimsanti hitaravat \|\| 3.3.37 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures distinguish the interchanges between the Lord and His devotees, as seen in sancari-bhava.**

**Комментарий:**

The interchange ‘I am You, You are me ’ heard from the scriptures should be known as a display of a state of rasa, such as a transitory emotion (sancari-bhava).

## \|\| 3.3.38 \|\|

*saiva hi satyadayah \|\| 3.3.38 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is superior, for qualities like truthfulness are automatically produced from her.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the difference between the spiritual practice of the jnanis and the bhaktas is shown. Bhakti automatically produces qualities like truthfulness, cleanliness, and so on, which the jnanis who aspire for liberation have to strive to attain.

## \|\| 3.3.39 \|\|

*kamaditaratra tatra cayatanadibhyah \|\| 3.3.39 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In vidhi-bhakti there is personal desire, while in raganuga-bhakti there is not. Even by vidhi-bhakti one attains liberation by engaging one’s home, family and so on in the Lord’s service.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who are expert in loving emotions consider that spiritual practice (sadhana) according to rules and regulations (vidhi-bhakti) is performed with personal desires, while that performed in spontaneous devotion (raganuga) is devoid of such desires. Even in the process of vidhi-bhakti, one attains liberation from this material world by knowing that one's home, family, etc., belong to the Supreme Lord. This is the pre-eminence of bhakti over jnana.

## \|\| 3.3.40 \|\|

*adarad alopah \|\| 3.3.40 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no loss by endeavoring for bhakti.**

**Комментарий:**

If many duties to be performed become simultaneously present, then there is no loss by simply endeavouring for bhakti. This proves the excellence of the activities of bhakti.

## \|\| 3.3.41 \|\|

*upasthite’tas tad-vacanat \|\| 3.3.41 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, according to the scriptural statements, if many duties have to be performed, then one should perform bhakti.**

**Комментарий:**

Therefore, according to the statements of the scriptures, if many duties to be performed become simultaneously present, then the activities of bhakti should be thoroughly performed.

## \|\| 3.3.42 \|\|

*tan-nirdharananiyamas tad-drsteh prthag ghy apratibandhah phalam \|\| 3.3.42 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Apart from the Lord’s view, there is uncertainty in ascertaining whether work should be performed or not. The fruit of work performed according to His will is not an obstacle.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the injunction for performing daily duties and so on is described. Work is executed by devotees like Yudhisthira, but not by devotees like Sukadeva. The reason is the Supreme Lord's desire. One who knows that this is the desire of the Supreme Lord performs his duties, but one who knows that the Lord does not desire this has no work to perform. When it is difficult to correctly ascertain the presence or not of the Lord's desire, being doubtful, one should engage in work. It should be known that the fruit of the work performed byAmbarisa and others who know the Lord's desire is the welfare of people rather than the attainment of the heavenly planets and so on. Similarly, the obstacles produced by giving up one's prescribed duties also disappear.

## \|\| 3. 3.43 \|\|

*pradanavad eva tad uktam \|\| 3. 3.43 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As stated in the scriptures, the Lord gives wholehearted devotion just as one who gives charity.**

**Комментарий:**

Wholehearted devotion (sarvatma-bhava) is given by the Lord Himself to the devotee. According to the statement starting with ‘nayam atma, ’ this depends on the Lord's choice. Just as charity is given to a brahmana without any motive, similarly wholehearted devotion is given by the Lord without seeing any cause. Although something may appear to be the cause for attaining it, that is not actually the cause. Therefore, wholehearted devotion is devoid of cause.

## \|\| 3.3.44 \|\|

*linga-bhuyastvat tad dhi baliyas tad api \|\| 3.3.44 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s selection is the most important factor, for there are several indications of it in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

For attaining wholehearted devotion, the Lord's choice is considered the most important factor, for several indications to this are seen in the Bhuma-vidya section. Oneness between the jiva and the impersonal Brahman is considered an obstacle and it is actually not possible, as it has been shown before in sutra 3,3.35. Wholehearted devotion is described as the awareness of Lord Hari everywhere and in everything. Acceptance by the Lord means to be selected by Him.

## \|\| 3.3.45 \|\|

*purva-vikalpah prakaranat syat kriya-manasavat \|\| 3.3.45 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“According to the context there, atma is first taught as the jiva and then as the Supreme Soul, just like external rituals and mental meditation are considered two modes of worship.”**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In the section on knowledge, the mode of the worship of the same non-different atma is described first as the jiva, and later as the Supreme Soul. Just like after having described external rituals, mental meditation is mentioned, which is a mode of worship, and not something different from it."

## \|\| 3.3.46 \|\|

*atidesac ca \|\| 3.3.46 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“And because names, speech, mind and so on are an extended aspect of Brahman.”**

**Комментарий:**

"And because in the passage starting with ‘namopassva’ (worship the names), names, speech, mind and so on are also similarly said to be an extended aspect of Brahman.

## \|\| 3.3.47 \|\|

*vidyaiva tu tan-nirdharanat \|\| 3.3.47 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is not true, for that is just a kind of vidya, as ascertained from the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

It is not so. What is called here as ‘sarvatma-bhava ’ is described as a kind of vidya, which is ascertained from the sruti ‘atmana evedam, ’ (everything emanates from the Supreme Soul). Considering the view of undifferentiated oneness, the atma or the impersonal Brahman could not be the source of everything. As for the assumption that it is understood from the context that this section is about jnana, it is not so, for it is seen that the topic is the Supreme Lord. The impersonal Brahman (aksara) is the abode, and the ruler is the Supreme Person. Thus, there is no contradiction between the previous and later statements in the sruti.

## \|\| 3.3.48 \|\|

*darsanac ca \|\| 3.3.48 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because this kind of wholehearted devotion is seen in the gopis.**

**Комментарий:**

I have also seen this kind of wholehearted devotion in the damsels of Vraja.

## \|\| 3.3.49 \|\|

*sruty-adi-baliyastvac ca na badhah \|\| 3.3.49 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the strength of direct scriptural statements (sruti) and indications (linga), there is no contradiction.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, ''According to the topic presented in the beginning of that section, this 'sarvatma-bhava ’ must be a different mode of Brahma-jnana. ” It is not so. On the strength of direct scriptural statements (sruti) and indications (linga) there, which are not contradicted by any sentence expressing it as a mode of Brahma-jnana, this is a kind of vidya.

## \|\| 3.3.50 \|\|

*anubandhadibhyah prajnantara-prthaktvavad drstas ca tad-uktam \|\| 3.3.50 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the connection of the words and so on. Just as there are different kinds of consciousness, so too there are different results. Such devotion is seen among the inhabitants of Vraja and it is expressed in the Bhuma-vidya section by the word 'mahima' (greatness).**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The indication that was shown about this ‘sarvatma-bhava" refers to the impersonal Brahman, which is manifest everywhere, for it expresses its non-dependence on anything on account of its allpervasiveness. ” In response to this, we say the following: When Gargi asked, “On what does Bhuma (the Supreme Being) rest?” then Yajhavalkya replied, “On His own greatness” (mahimni). Therefore, it is understood that the Supreme Lord is situated in ‘sarvatma-bhava" (wholehearted devotion). This is the connection. Such a connection is not possible if it is stated that He does not rest on anything at all. In this case, Yajhavalkya would have just replied, “Anywhere. ” Someone may argue, “Impersonal liberation comes after sarvatma-bhava. ” It is not so. Just as there are different kinds of consciousness, so too there are different results. This is seen among the inhabitants of Vraja. The ‘sarvatma-bhava" expressed here is understood as wholehearted devotion on account of the word ‘mahima " (greatness) mentioned in the sruti.

## \|\| 3.3.51 \|\|

*na samanyad apy upalabdher mrtyuvan na hi lokapattih \|\| 3.3.51 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This section is not about wholehearted devotion but about moving everywhere.” This is not true, for this is understood from the general attributes mentioned there. The liberated soul has no relation with the material planets just as it has no death.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “This section is not about wholehearted devotion, for it speaks about moving around all planets according to one's own will.” It is not so, for the employment of this term is understood from the general attributes mentioned there. Lord Hari is described in the sruti as being everything for His devotee. This confirms what has been previously stated, that just like one who takes shelter of the Supreme Being (Bhuma) does not see death, similarly he does not see the worlds as different from Him.

## \|\| 3.3.52 \|\|

*parena ca sabdasya tadvidhyam bhuyastvat tv anubandhah \|\| 3.3.52 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the connection of the previous and subsequent sentences, the meaning is clear. The emphasis is done on account of the multiplicity of statements.**

**Комментарий:**

From the previous and subsequent sentences, the meaning is very clearly understood: the Supreme Lord is everything for the devotee. The repetition of the meaning stated previously is emphasized on account of the multiplicity of statements, and therefore it is not superfluous.

## \|\| 3.3.53 \|\|

*eka atmanah sarire bhavat \|\| 3.3.53 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some meditate on the manifestations of the Supreme Soul within their bodies.**

**Комментарий:**

Some meditate on enjoyable objects of the Supreme Soul and His abode manifested within the body in the core of their hearts. What is not possible by the Supreme Lord's inconceivable potency?

## \|\| 3.3.54 \|\|

*vyatirekas tad-bhava-bhavitvan na tupalabdhivat \|\| 3.3.54 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord and His abode do not become manifest in the hearts of the jnanis in the same way as they acquire knowledge, for they have no devotion for Him.**

**Комментарий:**

In the minds of those who conceive the Supreme Lord as mere knowledge, there is no manifestation of the Supreme Lord's abode, for they have not been chosen by Him. Someone may argue, “The manifestation of Lord Hari and His abode should be similarly an object of knowledge for them in the core of their hearts. ” It is not so. Knowledge is attained by practice (sadhana), but not such a manifestation, which is dependent on the Lord's choice.

## \|\| 3.3.55 \|\|

*angavabaddhas tu na gakhasu hi prativedam \|\| 3.3.55 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Priests are bound to perform only one limb in a sacrifice, and not the functions of every Vedic branch.**

**Комментарий:**

The priests selected by the sacrificer perform functions in different capacities as appointed to them. Even if each of them is capable of performing the functions prescribed in any Vedic branch, still they do not act in any capacity for which they were not selected by the sacrificer. Similarly, those selected by the Supreme Lord to serve His feet will attain Him, and not others like the jnanis.

## \|\| 3.3.56 \|\|

*mantradivad vavirodhah \|\| 3.3.56 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no contradiction if one engaged in karma and jnana becomes a devotee, just as in the case of mantras and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Although engaged in performing their duties and cultivating knowledge, there are some who are selected by Lord Hari and thus attain devotion for Him. ” This is true. By the Supreme Lord's desire one may also attain devotion directly. Just like a mantra is sometimes employed in a particular ritual, sometimes in two rituals, and sometimes in three rituals. Therefore, there is no contradiction. Such cases should be understood according to the scriptures.

## \|\| 3.3.57 \|\|

*bhumnah kratuvaj jyayastvam tatha hi darsayati \|\| 3.3.57 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wholehearted devotion for the Supreme Lord is superior, just like Vedic sacrifices, for the scriptures demonstrate this.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The superiority of wholehearted devotion or love as previously stated is not possible, for that is also seen in the material world. ” It is not so. Wholehearted devotion for the Supreme Lord is indeed superior, for it is transcendental, just like articles such as a milk vessel in sacrifices like the Darsa-purnamasa. Something transcendental must be understood from the transcendental sruti. The same applies in this case too, for according to the truth understood from the sruti, wholehearted devotion for the Supreme Lord is superior to everything else, as previously stated.

## \|\| 3.3.58 \|\|

*nana sabdadibhedat \|\| 3.3.58 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are manifold forms of worship of the Lord’s multiple forms according to their respective mantras and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

The learned consider the worship of different forms of the Lord, like Krsna and Rama, as manifold according to the different mantras related to each of those forms.

## \|\| 3. 3.59 \|\|

*vikalpo’visista-phalatvat \|\| 3. 3.59 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One option should be adopted, for all of them yield the same result.**

**Комментарий:**

One option among various forms of worship is approved, never all of them combined. Since by a single form of worship one attains perfection, what is the purpose in adopting the other forms?

## \|\| 3.3.60 \|\|

*kamyas tu yatha-kamam samucciyeran na va purva-hetv-abhavat \|\| 3.3.60 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Worship motived by material desires may be combined or not as one wishes, for in this case, the previous motivation is not present.**

**Комментарий:**

The wise say that different forms of worship performed with the intention of fulfilling material desires should all be combined according to their different results, for only one may or may not fulfil all of one's desires.

## \|\| 3.3.61 \|\|

*angesu yathasraya-bhavah \|\| 3.3.61 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As for the limbs of different kinds of worship, they should be practised according to the worship they depend on.**

**Комментарий:**

Although their result is one and the same, different kinds of worship are mentioned in different places along with their limbs, regarding which there is the following consideration: a limb mentioned in connection with a particular kind of worship should be practised within that same worship.

## \|\| 3.3.62 \|\|

*sistes ca \|\| 3.3.62 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the instructions found in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

And because this is similarly understood from the instructions found in the srutis, smrtis, Puranas, and so on.

## \|\| 3.3.63 \|\|

*samaharat \|\| 3.3.63 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because all attributes are collectively present in one form of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

If the peaceful jnani meditates even on a single form of the Lord, then he combines all of His attributes in it.

## \|\| 3.3.64 \|\|

*guna-sadharanya-srutes ca \|\| 3.3.64 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the scriptures state that the Supreme Lord’s qualities are similarly present in all of His forms.**

**Комментарий:**

According to the scriptures, all qualities of the Supreme Lord such as supremacy and power are present in all of His forms.

## \|\| 3.3.65 \|\|

*na va tat-saha-bhavasruteh \|\| 3.3.65 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should worship multiple forms only if there is taste for it, otherwise not, for such a combined worship is not prescribed in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

One should regularly worship multiple forms of the Supreme Lord only if there is a particular taste for doing so, and not otherwise, for such a combined worship is not prescribed in the scriptures.

## \|\| 3.3.66 \|\|

*darsanac ca \|\| 3.3.66 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because even by worshipping a single form, one is able to see the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of worshipping the Supreme Lord is said to be His darsana, which is possible by the worship of even one of His forms. Therefore, one single form fulfils all desires and should be exclusively served.

## Section 4

## \|\| 3.4.1 \|\|

*purusartho’tah sabdad iti badarayanah \|\| 3.4.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vyasadeva says that according to the scriptures one attains the goal of life by devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

The ultimate goal of life, the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself is attained by wholehearted devotion (sarvatma-bhava), and not by karma and jnana, for this is the statement of the sruti, smrti and Puranas.

## \|\| 3.4.2 \|\|

*sesatvat purusartha-vado yathanyesv iti jaiminih \|\| 3.4.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Jaimini, the statements about bhakti are mere praise, as in the case of sacrificial paraphernalia, for devotion is subordinate to karma.**

**Комментарий:**

According to Jaimini, the scriptural statements about devotion to Lord Visnu should be understood as mere praise like that of a sacrificial ladle and so on, since it is subordinate to the performance of one's duty. Therefore, spiritual knowledge is dependent on karma.

## \|\| 3.4.3 \|\|

*acara-darsanat \|\| 3.4.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because this is seen in the conduct of the learned.**

**Комментарий:**

Spiritual knowledge is dependent on karma, for this is seen in the conduct of the learned.

## \|\| 3.4.4 \|\|

*tac chruteh \|\| 3.4.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because it is heard from the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

And because it is heard from the sruti.

## \|\| 3.4.5 \|\|

*samanvarambhanat \|\| 3.4.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because both knowledge and work act to produce results.**

**Комментарий:**

And because knowledge and work are seen together in the production of results.

## \|\| 3.4.6 \|\|

*tadvato vidhanat \|\| 3.4.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the injunction that one must have knowledge of Brahman to be appointed to perform a sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

One who has knowledge about Brahman is selected as a brahma priest. Therefore, it is understood that knowledge is subordinate to karma.

## \|\| 3.4.7 \|\|

*niyamac ca \|\| 3.4.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the precept to execute karma.**

**Комментарий:**

Fire sacrifices are prescribed for brahmanas so long as they live. Therefore, the performance of karma is not optional. Whatever was said above in praise of karma is thus the best thing.

## \|\| 3.4.8 \|\|

*adhikam tu badarayanasyaivam tad-darsanat \|\| 3.4.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The opinion of Vyasadeva is that knowledge is superior to karma, for this is seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Here Sri Vyasadeva shows the conclusion. Those who aspire for knowledge see that in the scriptures knowledge is superior to karma. Therefore, the conclusion is that karma is subordinate to knowledge.

## \|\| 3.4.9 \|\|

*tulyam tu darsanam \|\| 3.4.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is equally seen that some have given up karma.**

**Комментарий:**

The reason given that those who have spiritual knowledge perform karma is not absolute, for among them it is also seen that some have given up karma.

## \|\| 3.4.10 \|\|

*asarvatriki \|\| 3.4.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The quoted sruti passage is not universal.**

**Комментарий:**

As for the sruti quoted to corroborate the performance of karma, that is not considered a universal statement about all kinds of knowledge, for the renunciation of karma is also seen in many srutis.

## \|\| 3.4.11 \|\|

*vibhagah satavat \|\| 3.4.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Transcendentalists can be divided according to hundreds of gradations.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Although there is no difference in their spiritual knowledge, both renunciation and non-renunciation are exhibited, so how can there be any fixed rule in this matter?” This is what we say in response: One who has received great mercy from the Supreme Lord gives up karma completely and with indifference (nirapeksa). Someone else performs karma desiring the welfare of people. Just as there is no difference in spiritual bliss in any case, still its gradation can be divided into hundreds according to the Lord's desire. Similarly, there are many gradations amongst those who renounce work and those who do not.

## \|\| 3.4.12 \|\|

*adhyayana-matravatah \|\| 3.4.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The brahma priest is selected merely for expertise in reciting the Vedas.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the reply is given regarding what was previously stated about the brahma priest. The sruti says that the brahma priest must be ‘brahmistha, ’ which means he must be proficient in reciting the Vedas, not that he must know Brahman. He is selected as the sacrificial priest (brahma) because he is always engaged in studying the Vedas and is free from mundane desires. This being the meaning, it is not correct to say that knowledge is subordinate to karma.

## \|\| 3.4.13 \|\|

*navisesat \|\| 3.4.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Performance of work is not compulsory for all, for there is no such specification in the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as the precept to perform karma so long as one lives is mentioned in the sruti, so too is the principle that by its renunciation one attains liberation. No specification is mentioned in either case. Thus, the decision should be made according to the different status of life - householders should perform work, and sannyasis should renounce work.

## \|\| 3.4.14 \|\|

*stutaye’numatir va \|\| 3.4.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Then you agree that learned men are appointed to glorify karma.”**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “If by the word ‘brahmistha "you mean a jnani, then you agree that a learned man is appointed as priest to glorify the performance of karma. ”

## \|\| 3.4.15 \|\|

*kama-karena caike \|\| 3.4.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is not true, for some learned men may perform karma according to their own will.**

**Комментарий:**

"According to the sruti, the greatness of a learned person neither increases nor decreases by the performance of karma, yet they engage in it. Therefore, knowledge must be subordinate to karma. ”It is not so. The learned perform karma according to their own will in order to instruct people. Thus, there is no fault in the statements of the sruti.

## \|\| 3.4.16 \|\|

*upamardam ca \|\| 3.4.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By knowledge of the Supreme Lord the effects of karma are destroyed.**

**Комментарий:**

Others say that by knowledge of the Supreme Personality of Godhead all the effects of karma are destroyed.

## \|\| 3.4.17 \|\|

*urdhva-retahsu ca sabdehi \|\| 3.4.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among celibates, no karma is performed, and from the scriptures it is understood that knowledge is not dependent upon it.**

**Комментарий:**

Since no karma is performed in the celibate asramas, it is understood that knowledge is not dependent on karma. Someone may argue, "The knowledge acquired by one in the renounced order is subordinate to the karma prescribed for that order of life. ” It is not so. Knowledge and its fruit - liberation - are understood only from the scriptures, where the nature of both is described.

## \|\| 3.4.18 \|\|

*paramarsam jaiminir acodana capavadati hi \|\| 3.4.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaimini thinks that karma should be performed and condemns its renunciation, for there is no injunction to give it up.**

**Комментарий:**

Jaimini says that by giving up karma one cannot attain liberation, for in the Vedas there is no injunction to give up karma, and because this is reproachable.

## \|\| 3.4.19 \|\|

*anustheyam badarayanah samya-sruteh \|\| 3.4.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vyasadeva denies that karma should be compulsorily performed, for there are similar scriptural statements that condemn karma.**

**Комментарий:**

In the scriptures there are similar statements that condemn karma, therefore Vyasadeva clearly denies that the performance of karma is compulsory.

## \|\| 3.4.20 \|\|

*vidhir va dharanavat \|\| 3.4.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The injunction to perform karma is a subordinate limb, just like steadiness of the mind in order to attain samadhi.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as steadiness of the mind is prescribed in order to attain samadhi, similarly the injunction to perform karma is considered as a limb subordinate to jnana and bhakti.

## \|\| 3.4.21 \|\|

*stuti-matram upadanad iti cen napurvatvat \|\| 3.4.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that the aforementioned sruti is merely a glorification of the jnanis, we say that this is not true, for that is an original injunction.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The statement in the sruti that the greatness of a learned person does not increase or decrease by the performance of karma is mere praise, for by such a statement it is implied that the jnani does take to its performance. Therefore, knowledge is subordinate to karma. ” It is not so. It is understood that the activity of one who has spiritual knowledge is not tainted by the results of karma. The meaning of that passage is to be taken as an original injunction, therefore the idea that the sruti is merely praising the jnanis is refuted.

## \|\| 3.4.22 \|\|

*bhava-sabdac ca \|\| 3.4.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the Vedic statements corroborate that spiritual knowledge is independent.**

**Комментарий:**

From the Vedic injunction that one should leave home and take sannyasa, it is understood that spiritual knowledge is independent.

## \|\| 3.4.23 \|\|

*pariplavartha iti cen na visesitatvat \|\| 3.4.23 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that narrations about spiritual knowledge are meant for pdriplava, hence subordinate to karma, we say that this is not true, for knowledge is superior to karma.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "From sentences like ‘pariplave samsanti’ (they tell the narrations during the pariplava) it is understood that the spiritual knowledge seen in the narrative portions of the sruti is subordinate to karma. ’’Please do not speak so, for in the non-narrative portions of the sruti, knowledge is said to be superior to karma.

## \|\| 3.4.24 \|\|

*tatha caika-vakyatopabandhat \|\| 3.4.24 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Furthermore, it is so because there is a coherent connection between the narrations and instructions therein.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Knowledge is heard both in the narrative portion as well as in the non-narrative portion of the scriptures, so what is the use of the narrative portion?” Listen: The sruti says that one who has a preceptor should pursue knowledge. Without narrations about the preceptor and the disciple, the coherence of such scriptural statements would not be demonstrated. Thus, there is a connection between the narrations and the instructions therein.

## \|\| 3.4.25 \|\|

*ata eva cagnindhanady-anapeksa \|\| 3.4.25 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, the jnani does not need to perform rituals like igniting the sacrificial fire, and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

Because the jnani himself turns into sacrifice personified by dint of knowledge, there is no need of Vedic rituals performed by igniting the sacrificial fire.

## \|\| 3.4.26 \|\|

*sarvapeksa ca yajnadi-sruter asvavat \|\| 3.4.26 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As sacrifices and other rituals are mentioned in the scriptures, all those ritualistic activities are required to attain knowledge, just as one needs a horse to reach a destination.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In this way the Karma-kanda portion of the Vedas would be useless. ” Please do not speak so. All ritualistic activities are required to produce knowledge in this world, for the description of sacrifices and so on is mentioned in the scriptures. Once knowledge is produced, there is no such requirement. Just as a horse is required to reach a destination, mot after reaching it, similarly the injunctions to perform karma should be followed until one attains knowledge, after which they are given up.

## \|\| 3,4.27 \|\|

*sama-damady-upetah syat tathapi tu tad-vidhes tad-angataya tesam avasyanustheyatvat \|\|3,4.27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Yet one should be endowed with peacefulness, self-control and so on, for according to the injunctions these qualities are secondary limbs of knowledge and must be practised.**

**Комментарий:**

Although one attains knowledge by the performance of ritualistic activities like sacrifices, still it is understood that one who aspires for knowledge should be endowed with qualities like peacefulness, self-control and the like, because they are secondary limbs of knowledge. This principle is seen in the sruti and the smrti, therefore qualities like self-control should be practised.

## \|\| 3.4.28 \|\|

*sarvannanumatis ca pranatyaye tad-darsanat \|\| 3.4.28 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In case of danger to one’s life, there is permission to take all kinds of foodstuffs, as seen in the case of the sage Cakrayana.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “It is not correct that one's existence is purified by peacefulness, self-control, and so on, for in the scriptures we see discrepancies regarding pure food, which is also a cause of purity. " It is not so. It should be understood that the sage Cakrayana accepted any kind of food because his life was at risk.

## \|\| 3.4.29 \|\|

*abadhac ca \|\| 3.4.29 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because there is no obstruction of knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

It is heard from the scriptures that at a time of calamity, there is no fault in eating all kinds of foodstuffs. By doing so, there is no contamination in one's consciousness and there is no obstruction of one's acquisition of knowledge.

## \|\| 3.4.30 \|\|

*api smaryate \|\| 3.4.30 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is also so recollected by the sages.**

**Комментарий:**

This is also so recollected by the sages.

## \|\| 3.4.31 \|\|

*sabdas cato’kama-care \|\| 3.4.31 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In other situations, the scriptures forbid acting without restraint.**

**Комментарий:**

When one is in good health, the scriptures forbid a learned man fixed in spiritual practice from acting without restraint.

## \|\| 3.4.32 \|\|

*vihitatvac casrama-karmapi \|\| 3.4.32 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should also perform the work of his respective asrama, for this is prescribed in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as a learned person should not eat impure foodstuffs when there is no adversity, similarly he should also perform the work of his respective asrama, for this is prescribed in the scriptures.

## \|\| 3.4.33 \|\|

*saha-karitvena ca \|\| 3.4.33 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The duties of one’s asrama should be performed as an auxiliary to cultivate peacefulness, self-control, and so on.**

**Комментарий:**

The learned should perform the duties prescribed for their respective asrama as an auxiliary to the cultivation of peacefulness, self-control, and so on.

## \|\| 3.4.34 \|\|

*sarvathapi ta evobhaya-lingat \|\| 3.4.34 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Devotees should completely engage in devotional activities, for the evidence to this is seen both in the sruti and the smrti.**

**Комментарий:**

After declaring the mode of action for the jnanis, now the action of the devotees is shown. If the activities of the path of jnana and the path of bhakti need to be simultaneously performed, then the dear devotees of Lord Hari should completely perform devotional duties, for the evidence to this is seen in the sruti and the smrti.

## \|\| 3.435 \|\|

*anabhibhavam ca darsayati \|\| 3.435 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti shows that devotees are not overcome by the faults of not performing work**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti shows that a devotee of Lord Hari is not overcome by the faults caused by not performing one’s prescribed work.

## \|\| 3.4.36 \|\|

*antara capi tu tad-drsteh \|\| 3. 4.36 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The varnasrama rules are considered by the devotee as an obstacle to see Lord Krsna.**

**Комментарий:**

The varnasrama rules prescribed in the sruti are seen by the devotee as an obstacle to seeing Lord Krsna.

## \|\| 3.4.37 \|\|

*api smaryate \|\| 3.4.37 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is so stated in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

It is so stated in the smrtis and other scriptures.

## \|\| 3.4.38 \|\|

*visesanugrahas ca \|\| 3.4.38 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees attain the Supreme Lord’s special favour.**

**Комментарий:**

The result attained by the jnanis is mere liberation, while the result attained by the devotees is the Supreme Lord's special favour in the form of coming under their control.

## \|\| 3.4.39 \|\|

*atas tv itaraj jyayo lingac ca \|\| 3.4.39 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, according to the evidence in the scriptures, to become a devotee is better than liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Because of the reason stated above, to become dear to the Supreme Lord is better than impersonal liberation. This is confirmed by the evidence in the sruti and the smrti.

## \|\| 3.4.40 \|\|

*tad-bhutasya tu natad-bhavo jaiminer api niyamatad-rupabhavebhyah \|\| 3.4.40 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even Jaimini agrees that a devotee can never be otherwise on account of engaging the senses in the Lord and having no other desire than serving Him.**

**Комментарий:**

One who has become dear to the Supreme Lord can never become otherwise, for the scriptures declare that the devotees’ senses are engaged in the Lord and they have no other desire than to serve Him. Even Jaimini agrees with this.

## \|\| 3.4.41 \|\|

*na cadhikarikam api patananumanat tad-ayogat \|\| 3.4.41 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Considering that unbefitting for them and thinking about their fall from that position, Lord Krsna does not give the post of demigods to His dear devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Considering that unbefitting for them and thinking about their fall from that position, Lord Krsna does not give His dear devotees results such as the posts of exalted demigods, but He always grants them His own lotus feet.

## \|\| 3.4.42 \|\|

*upapurvam api tv eke bhavam asanavat tad uktam \|\| 3.4.42 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is said that situated in whatever position, the devotees enjoy the pleasure of love for God.**

**Комментарий:**

Wherever they may be situated, the dear devotees of Lord Kesava, intoxicated with love, consider the pleasures of that place like that of Vaikuntha, having love for God as their supreme object.

## \|\| 3.4.43 \|\|

*bahis tubhayathapi smrter acarac ca \|\| 3.4.43 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees are situated both inside and outside this world because of their remembrance of the Supreme Lord and their uncommon behaviour.**

**Комментарий:**

Although the devotees are situated within the material world, still it should be known that they are out of it, for having attained the wealth of love, they always remember their union with the Lord and display an uncommon behaviour.

## \|\| 3.4.44 \|\|

*svaminah phala-sruter ity atreyah \|\| 3.4.44 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sage Atreya, the sruti declares that Lord Krsna maintains His devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Lord Krsna maintains the bodily needs of His devotees. So says the sage Atreya, who knows the essence of the truth expressed in the Sruti.

## \|\| 3.4.45 \|\|

*artvijyam ity audulomis tasmai hi parikriyate \|\| 3.4.45 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Audulomi, being purchased by devotion, the Supreme Lord does everything for His devotee, just like a hired priest performs a sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as a hired priest performs all sacrificial rituals, similarly Lord Hari, being purchased by devotion, does everything for His devotee.

## \|\| 3.4.46 \|\|

*saha-kary-antara-vidhih paksena trtiyam tadvato vidhy-adivat \|\| 3.4.46 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The injunction about different auxiliaries meant for thejnanis has been stated. A third auxiliary is like an injunction meant for the exclusively dedicated devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

It has been previously stated that peacefulness, self-control and so on are different auxiliaries. This injunction is to be followed by the jnanis. Now a third sadhana is shown, which is to be practised by the devotees within their minds. According to the exalted souls, just as the injunctions about performing the sandhya worship and so on are desirable for the jnanis, similarly meditation is the approved sadhana for the exclusively dedicated devotees.

## \|\| 3.4.47 \|\|

*krtsna-bhavat tu grhinopasamharah \|\| 3.4.47 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The discussion about knowledge is concluded by describing the duties of household life because it includes the duties of all asramas.**

**Комментарий:**

The Chandogya Upanisad concludes the section about knowledge by describing the duties of the householders. The learned should understand that this is because the household duties include those of all the four asramas. Therefore, the path of renunciation is not discarded. Yet household life should be adopted when one lacks renunciation. This is the meaning of the instruction of the scriptures.

## \|\| 3.4.48 \|\|

*maunavad itaresam apy upadesat \|\| 3.4.48 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The duties of all asramas, such as silence, are included in the household asrama, for this is taught in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Just like silence, freedom from desires, and so on are considered duties for a sannydsi, they are similarly meant for the householders too. Therefore, the duties of all asramas are included in the asrama of householders.

## \|\| 3.4.49 \|\|

*anaviskurvann anvayat \|\| 3.4.49 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee should worship the Lord without outward displays of love, for this is the teaching received from His followers.**

**Комментарий:**

A devotee should worship the Supreme Lord without revealing his love outwardly, just like an intelligent beggar does not display the money he receives, for this is the teaching in our sampradaya.

## \|\| 3. 4.50 \|\|

*aihikam aprastuta-pratibandhe tad-darsanat \|\| 3. 4.50 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee should engage only in worldly activities that are not an impediment to devotional service, for this is shown by exalted devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

A devotee should perform only worldly activities that are not an impediment to the Lord's worship, for this is shown by the conduct of His dearest devotees.

## \|\| 3.4.51 \|\|

*evam mukti-phalaniyamas tad-avasthavadhrtes tad-avasthavadhrteh \|\| 3.4.51 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are no fixed rules regarding the fruits of liberation, for the stages of liberation are ascertained as different.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as there are different kinds of regulations for sadhana, similarly one should know the difference according to the fruits of liberation, for the stage of liberation is ascertained as different in every case.

## Chapter 4

## Section 1

## \|\| 4.1.1 \|\|

*avrttir asakrd-upadesat \|\| 4.1.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service should be repeatedly practised, for the instructions are often repeated.**

**Комментарий:**

One should constantly repeat the processes of devotional service such as hearing and chanting until they yield fruit, for this is repeatedly taught in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.1.2 \|\|

*lingac ca \|\| 4.1.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because of the evidence in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

And because the repetition of hearing, chanting and so on is known from the evidence in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.1.3 \|\|

*atmeti tupagacchanti grahayanti ca \|\| 4.1.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The knowers of the Vedas worship Lord Hari as the Supreme Soul and teach this to their disciples.**

**Комментарий:**

The best knowers of the Vedas worship Lord Hari as the Supreme Soul, and they teach this to their good disciples, who therefore attain liberation.

## \|\| 4.1.4 \|\|

*na pratikena hi sah \|\| 4.1.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There cannot be liberation by worship of symbols.**

**Комментарий:**

By worshipping a symbol one can never attain liberation.

## \|\| 4.1.5 \|\|

*brahma-drstir utkarsat \|\| 4.1.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord should be seen as being present everywhere, for this is a superior vision.**

**Комментарий:**

To see the Supreme Lord as being present everywhere is actually a superior vision, by which one's liberation is totally assured.

## \|\| 4.1.6 \|\|

*adityadi-matayas canga upapatteh \|\| 4.1.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should meditate on the sun, etc., as limbs of the Supreme Lord, for this is appropriate.**

**Комментарий:**

One should meditate on the sun, etc., as different limbs of the Supreme Lord, which are transcendentally pure like Him, being His parts. By such a meditation one attains liberation.

## \|\| 4.1.7 \|\|

*asinah sambhavat \|\| 4.1.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord sits before a devotee, for this is possible when there is love for God.**

**Комментарий:**

It was stated that to see the manifestation of the impersonal Brahman everywhere is the result attained by the jnanis. Now it is stated that to see the manifestation of Lord Krsna is the result attained by the devotees. The Supreme Lord Hari sits before a devotee who meditates with love, for such a manifestation is possible when there is love for God.

## \|\| 4.1.8 \|\|

*dhyanac ca \|\| 4.1.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And also in their hearts because of their meditation.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord also sits in the heart of His devotees because of their meditation.

## \|\| 4.1.9 \|\|

*acalatvam capeksya \|\| 4.1.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Considering the devotee’s desire, the Supreme Lord moves, stands still, and does anything.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord is made a lover by their love. Considering the devotees ’ desire, He moves, stands still, and does all kinds of things.

## \|\| 4.1.10 \|\|

*smaranti ca \|\| 4.1.10  \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sages also recollect so.**

**Комментарий:**

The sages also recollect so.

## \|\| 4.1.11 \|\|

*yatraikagrata tatravisesat \|\| 4.1.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wherever the mind is fixed, there the Supreme Lord becomes manifest without any difference.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord's manifestation is twofold - internal and external. There is no difference between them, for the difference is only the place in which the mind is fixed.

## \|\| 4.1.12 \|\|

*aprayanat tatrapi hi drstam \|\| 4.1.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee sees the Supreme Lord internally and externally until liberation, and even after that.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord manifests Himself in the same form internally and externally until liberation, after which He awards the devotee the ultimate result -eternal service to His lotus feet.

## \|\| 4.1.13 \|\|

*tad-adhigama uttara-purvaghayor aslesa-vinasau tad-vyapadesat \|\| 4.1.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When spiritual knowledge arises, the jnani's past and future reactions are destroyed, for this is mentioned in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

When spiritual knowledge arises, then the enlightened jnanis attain the stage of jivan-mukti. Their previous sinful reactions are destroyed, and future reactions do not touch them, for this is seen in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.1.14 \|\|

*itarasyapyevam asamslesah pate tu \|\| 4.1.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When both pious and impious reactions are destroyed, the activities of the devotees do not bind them.**

**Комментарий:**

The position regarding pious activities is the same as sinful ones. It is also heard from the scriptures that when both are destroyed by the will of the Supreme Lord, the activities of the devotees in vidhi-bhakti do not bind them.

## \|\| 4.1.15 \|\|

*anarabdha-karya eva tu purve tad-avadheh \|\| 4.1.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only the reactions which are not manifest are destroyed. Those which are manifest remain so long as the devotee lives.**

**Комментарий:**

Only the pious and sinful reactions which are not yet manifest (anarabdha) are destroyed at once. By Lord Hari's will, the reactions which are manifest (arabdha) remain so long as the devotee lives. After they are destroyed, one attains final liberation.

## \|\| 4.1.16 \|\|

*agnihotradi tu tat-karyayaiva tad-darsanat \|\| 4.1.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Fire sacrifices and so on are meant to decrease one’s manifest karma, for this is seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The performance of fire sacrifices and so on is meant for decreasing the jivan-mukta's manifest karma, and not to produce new fruits, for this is seen in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.1.17 \|\|

*ato’nyapi hy ekesam ubhayoh \|\| 4.1.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is another sruti that states that both the manifest and nonmanifest reactions of the rdganuga devotees are all destroyed.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the distinction of the raganuga devotees is shown. Both the manifest and non-manifest pious and sinful reactions of those who have spontaneous love for Lord Krsna are destroyed without being experienced, for these are the statements found in other srutis apart from those which state that the reactions are destroyed after being enjoyed.

## \|\| 4.1.18 \|\|

*yad eva vidyayeti hi \|\| 4.1.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The destruction of karma is possible by knowledge, for it is the Supreme Lord’s energy.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowledge (vidya) is a part of the Supreme Lord s energy. If by this energy very powerful works are possible, then the destruction of karma is not difficult for it.

## \|\| 4.1.19 \|\|

*bhogena tv itare ksapayitvatha sampadyate \|\| 4.1.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the subtle and gross bodies, the raganuga devotee enjoys supreme bliss.**

**Комментарий:**

By Lord Hari's mercy, a great raganuga devotee gives up the subtle and gross bodies and enjoys supreme bliss.

## Section 2

## \|\| 4.2.1 \|\|

*van manasi darsanac chabdac ca \|\| 4.2.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By seeing the Supreme Lord and hearing the sound of His flute within the mind, the speech merges in it.**

**Комментарий:**

At the time when the previously mentioned raganuga devotees attain the Supreme Lord, by seeing Him and hearing the sound of His flute, their speech and the other senses merge into the mind, the mind merges into the life-air, and the lifeair merges into the Supreme Soul. This is the medium. Due to the vision of Sri Krsna-candra and the sound of His flute within the mind, the devotee's speech then becomes perfect and converges with the mind.

## \|\| 4.2.2 \|\|

*ata eva sarvany anu \|\| 4.2.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After the speech, all the other senses also merge in the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

After the speech merges in the mind, all the other senses of the raganuga devotee also merge in it for the above-mentioned reason.

## \|\| 4.2.3 \|\|

*tan manah prana uttarat \|\| 4.2.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From the subsequent statement in the sruti, the mind merges in the life-air.**

**Комментарий:**

Then the mind endowed with all senses merges in the life-air. The wise know this from the subsequent statement in the sruti.

## \|\| 4.2.4 \|\|

*so’dhyakse tad-upagamadibhyah \|\| 4.2.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The life-air then merges with the Supreme Soul out of His choice and affection.**

**Комментарий:**

The life-air accompanied by all the senses and the mind then directly merges into the Supreme Soul on account of having been chosen by Lord Krsna and having His affection.

## \|\| 4.2.5 \|\|

*bhutesu tac-chruteh \|\| 4.2.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sruti, the jnani's senses merge into the great elements.**

**Комментарий:**

The senses of the jnanis merge into the great elements, for the sruti beginning with ‘yatrasya purusasya' clearly teaches this.

## \|\| 4.2.6 \|\|

*naikasmin darsayato hi \|\| 4.2.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord does not give vidhi-bhakti only to His devotees, but to the jnanis as well, for this is taught in the conversation between Yajnavalkya and Artabhaga.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Sometimes the Supreme Lord gives devotional service according to rules and regulations (vidhi-bhakti) to ajnani, and sometimes He gives spontaneous devotional service to a devotee in vidhi-bhakti. ” It is not so. Sometimes He may accept a jnani to perform service according to vidhi-bhakti. Therefore, the conclusion is that service in vidhi-bhakti can be given to both of them, and not only to one. This is seen in the conversation between Yajnavalkya and Artabhaga. The Supreme Lord never brings any of these two into the path of spontaneous devotion but sometimes gives them vidhi-bhakti, which is expressed there by the word "karma. ’

## \|\| 4.2.7 \|\|

*samana casrty-upakramad amrtatvam canuposya \|\| 4.2.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The same rules are observed from the very beginning in the path of vidhi-bhakti. In the path of raganuga-bhakti, one attains liberation without observing rules.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "If the rule is so, then what is the cause of vidhi-bhakti and raganuga-bhakti?" This is what we say: The nature of both is similar. Becoming separated from the Supreme Lord, the jiva has come to this material world. When, from that time up to liberation, one worships Lord Hari, then one is freed from samsara. This same rule is seen throughout the path of vidhi-bhakti. In the path of raganuga-bhakti, without performing vows, and so on, one attains liberation by the mercy of the Supreme Lord.

## \|\| 4.2.8 \|\|

*tadapiteh samsara-vyapadesat \|\| 4.2.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the raganuga devotees attain the fruit of spontaneous love for God, they consider vidhi-bhakti as similar to samsara.**

**Комментарий:**

How can vidhi-bhakti be expressed by the word ‘karma’ as used in the conversation between Yajnavalkya and Artabhaga? The answer is stated here: At the time when the raganuga devotees attain the fruit of spontaneous love, they regard the liberation of those on the path of vidhi-bhakti as 'samsara.' That is why vidhi-bhakti was called karma in the conversation between both sages.

## \|\| 4.2.9 \|\|

*suksmam pramanatas ca tathopalabdheh \|\| 4.2.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the evidence found in the scriptures, the path of raganuga-bhakti is very difficult to understand.**

**Комментарий:**

The best among the learned consider the nature of the path of raganuga-bhakti very difficult to understand. This is also concluded from the evidence found in the scriptures; hence this path is followed only by rare individuals.

## \|\| 4.2.10 \|\|

*nopamardenatah \|\| 4.2.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Teaching about raganuga-bhakti is not possible, either because one is afflicted by separation or overcome by loving exchanges.**

**Комментарий:**

There are two stages in the path of raganuga-bhakti - separation and union. In separation, teaching is not possible because one is overwhelmed by feelings of aisvarya-bhava. In union, teaching is also not possible because one is overcome by loving exchanges. Therefore, the truth about raganuga-bhakti does not spread in all directions.

## \|\| 4.2.11 \|\|

*asyaiva copapatter usma \|\| 4.2.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such distress is also bliss, for this is logical.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "How can separation from the supreme bliss personified be a cause of manifold distress?” The conclusion about this is now been stated here: since bliss is an attribute of the Supreme Lord, Who is bliss personified, it is logical that the distress caused by separation from Him must also be a form of supreme bliss.

## \|\| 4.2.12 \|\|

*pratisedhad iti cen na sarirat \|\| 4.2.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone argues that the scriptures deny that a devotee can feel distress, we say that this is not true, for it refers only to mundane distress.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, "Transitory emotions like despondency, weakness and anxiety are not possible, for the scriptures deny that such a devotee can feel distress and so on.” It is not so. Because the sruti text also mentions freedom from disease, the intended meaning must be only bodily distress. Why should a form of transitory bliss be excluded?

## \|\| 4.2.13 \|\|

*spasto hy ekesam \|\| 4.2.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to some Vedic followers, this rasa is a clear form of happiness.**

**Комментарий:**

According to the followers of a certain Vedic branch, this rasa appears as a clear form of happiness, and therefore it is nothing like mundane distress.

## \|\| 4.2.14 \|\|

*smaryate ca \|\| 4.2.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The smrtis also corroborate this.**

**Комментарий:**

In the smrtis like the Srimad-Bhagavatam it is also stated so.

## \|\| 4.2.15 \|\|

*tani pare tatha hy aha \|\| 4.2.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures state that the rarest things are available in the supreme land of Vraja.**

**Комментарий:**

The sruti and the smrti say that those things that are rarely attained even in Vaikuntha are available everywhere in the most exalted area of Vraja.

## \|\| 4.2.16 \|\|

*avibhago vacanat \|\| 4.2.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the statements of the scriptures, the devotee is never separated from Lord Krsna’s lotus-feet.**

**Комментарий:**

According to the statements of the scriptures, when Lord Krsna accepts a devotee, then such a devotee is never again separated from Krsna's lotus-feet.

## \|\| 4.2.17 \|\|

*tad-oko’gra-jvalanam tat-prakasita-dvaro vidya-samarthyat tac-chesa-gaty-anusmrti-yogac ca hardanugrhitah satadhikaya \|\| 4.2.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the upper portion of the heart shines and the path is illuminated, the jiva who receives the mercy of the Supreme Lord within the heart on account of the acquired knowledge, by which it constantly thinks on the Lord, then leaves the body through the susumna.**

**Комментарий:**

It has been previously stated that the senses of the jnani combine with the great elements. Now the departure of the jnani already devoid of senses is explained. When the jiva is leaving the body, the upper portion of the heart shines. Having its path illuminated, the jiva then comes out of the body. A jnani who is favoured by the Supreme Lord goes out through the susumna. This is the distinction of a jnani in comparison with a mundane person. One's competence in knowledge is said to be the cause of the Supreme Lord's mercy. Constant thought on Lord Visnu is attained as a result of knowledge.

## \|\| 4.2.18 \|\|

*rasmy-anusari \|\| 4.2.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise attain the Supreme Lord via the rays of the sun.**

**Комментарий:**

Only the wise attain Lord Visnu's nature via the rays of the sun.

## \|\| 4.2.19 \|\|

*nisi neti cen na sambandhasyayavad-deha-bhavitvad darsayati ca \|\| 4.2.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one objects that this is not possible at night, we say that this is not true, for so long as the body lasts, the jnani is connected with the Supreme Soul by remembering Him. This is seen in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “On account of the absence of sun rays, the jnani cannot depart from the body at night." It is not so, for so long as the body lasts, the jnani is connected with the Supreme Soul by constantly remembering Him. Therefore, there is no hindrance to attain Lord Hari in any case. The sruti and the smrti clearly state this fact.

## \|\| 4.2.20 \|\|

*atas cayane’pi daksine \|\| 4.2.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even during the southern course of the sun, the jnani departs from this world and attains the supreme abode.**

**Комментарий:**

Even the jnani who dies during the southern course of the sun leaves this world and attains the supreme abode.

## \|\| 4.2.21 \|\|

*yoginah prati smaryate smarte caite \|\| 4.2.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The two paths mentioned in the Bhagavad-gita are for the followers of Samkhya and Yoga.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “In the Bhagavad-gita it is stated that there are two paths, hence the jnani is restricted to a specific time and movement of the sun." It is not so because this is stated for the followers of Samkhya and Yoga. Therefore, under no circumstance can this apply to those who know the Supreme Lord. In the case of Bhisma, he waited for a particular time to die to prove the efficacy of the boon he received.

## Section 3

## \|\| 4.3.1 \|\|

*arcir-adina tat-prathiteh \|\| 4.3.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jndni goes through the path of light, for this is well-known as the path to the spiritual world.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord declares that the steps starting with light is the path through which the jnani attains Him after leaving the body. A jnani who has given up the body goes through the path of light to the eternal Supreme Brahman, for this is well-known as the path to reach it.

## \|\| 4.3.2 \|\|

*vayum abdad avisesa-visesabhyam \|\| 4.3.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vayu should be included after the year, for steps are included based on whether they have been specified or not.**

**Комментарий:**

Vayu (air) is not mentioned in the path of light in the Chandogya Upanisad, but it is seen after Agni (fire) in the Kausitaki-brahmana Upanisad. Since both are the same path, Vayu is to be included somewhere in the steps. The wise should include it between Samvatsara (year) and Aditya (sun), which are mentioned in the Chandogya Upanisad. The conclusion here is based on whether a particular step has been specified or not.

## \|\| 4.3.3 \|\|

*tadito’dhi varunah sambandhat \|\| 4.3.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Varuna must be the step after Vidyut because of their relation to water.**

**Комментарий:**

Varuna should be understood as the step after Vidyut (lightning), for it is observed that the relation of both to water indicates this.

## \|\| 4.3.4 \|\|

*varunac cadhindra-prajapati \|\| 4.3.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Indra and Brahma should also be included after Varuna.**

**Комментарий:**

The demigods Indra and Prajapati Brahma should be understood by the learned as the steps after Varuna because of their relation as heads of the hosts of demigods. Arci (light), Dina (day), Sukla-paksa (bright-fortnight), Uttarayana (the northern course of the sun), Vatsara (year), Vayu (air), Surya (sun), Candra (moon), Vidyut (lightning), Varuna (water), Indra and Brahma - these are the twelve steps on the path that starts with light.

## \|\| 4.3.5 \|\|

*ativahikas tal-lingat \|\| 4.3.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All these are demigods who carry the soul to the spiritual abode, for this is indicated in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who go to the spiritual abode through the path of light come in contact with all these demigods who carry them through the space. After that, a spiritual being comes to carry those worshippers.

## \|\| 4.3.6 \|\|

*ubhaya-vyamohat tat-siddheh \|\| 4.3.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Being bewildered by the Lord, the jnanis and the vidhi-bhaktas see the higher planets in order to fulfil this desire.**

**Комментарий:**

Although giving up instant liberation, the jnanis and vidhi-bhaktas would need to follow the path of light, which does not befit them, still by the Supreme Lord's will it is possible that they become bewildered by the desire to see the higher planets, and thus such a desire is fulfilled. “If this path is seen by the best of transcendentalists, then all their deeds are fruitful, ” thinking thus, the Supreme Lord of all the demigods inspires them with such a desire.

## \|\| 4.3.7 \|\|

*vaidyutenaiva tatas tac-chruteh \|\| 4.3.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sruti, from Vidyut the jnani is carried by an attendant of Lord Visnu and then attains a spiritual form.**

**Комментарий:**

An attendant of Lord Visnu comes up to Vidyut to carry that wise soul. According to the sruti, one then attains a spiritual form.

## \|\| 4.3.8 \|\|

*karyam badarir asya gaty-upapatteh \|\| 4.3.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Badari, the destination of the path of light is Lord Brahma, for it is possible to go to him.**

**Комментарий:**

Now some deliberation is made whether the jnani travelling on the path that starts with light attains the Supreme Brahman or its effect, Lord Brahma. Badari says that one must attain its effect, Lord Brahma, for going is possible in this case.

## \|\| 4.3.9 \|\|

*visesitatvac ca \|\| 4.3.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because it is specified in the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

Because in many places in the sruti the abode of Lord Brahma is specified as the destination.

## \|\| 4.3.10 \|\|

*samipyat tu tad-vyapadesah \|\| 4.3.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma’s name is due to his proximity to the Supreme Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

After the abode of Lord Brahma, one attains the Supreme Brahman, by whose proximity Lord Brahma is suitably named so.

## \|\| 4.3.11 \|\|

*karyatyaye tad-adhyaksena sahatah param abhidhanat \|\| 4.3.11 \|\|*

## \|\| 4.3.12 \|\|

*smrtes ca \|\| 4.3.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the time of universal dissolution, the inhabitants of Brahma-Ioka accompanied by Lord Brahma attain the Supreme Lord, for it is so stated in the sruti and the smrti.**

**Комментарий:**

When the effect (Brahma-Ioka) is destroyed, then those who live there together with Lord Brahma attain the Supreme Lord, for this is stated in the sruti and the smrti. Thus, there is no contradiction in the statement that from Brahma-Ioka there is no return again to this world.

## \|\| 4.3.13 \|\|

*param jaiminir mukhyatvat \|\| 4.3.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaimini says that the destination is the Supreme Brahman itself, for this is the primary meaning of the word ‘brahman.’**

**Комментарий:**

On the basis of the meaning of the word ‘brahman ’ in that passage, Jaimini says that the Supreme Brahman itself is attained by the path of light.

## \|\| 4.3.14 \|\|

*darsanac ca \|\| 4.3.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And because the sruti says so.**

**Комментарий:**

And because according to the sruti, the destination is different than Prajapati Brahma’s abode.

## \|\| 4.3.15 \|\|

*na ca karye pratipatty-abhisandhih \|\| 4.3.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise do not have the intention to attain Lord Brahma’s abode.**

**Комментарий:**

Attaining the spiritual world, the wise obtain great enjoyment. It is not reasonable that they would have the intention of obtaining such enjoyment in Lord Brahma's abode, for according to the sruti and the smrti, only the spiritual world is imperishable.

## \|\| 4.3.16 \|\|

*apratikalambanan nayatiti badarayana ubhayatha ca dosat tat-kratus ca \|\| 4.3.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Vyasadeva, Lord Visnu’s attendant brings only those who do not resort to symbolic worship, for there are two kinds of fault in it. A devotee spontaneously goes to the supreme abode.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who worship the Supreme Lord knowing that He is everything are brought by His attendant to the supreme abode via the path that starts with light, but not those who resort to symbolic worship, for they have a firm belief that the Supreme Lord is not transcendental, and at the time of worship they consider something material as transcendental. Because of these two faults, they do not go to the supreme abode. Vyasadeva says that this is the meaning of the sruti. On the other hand, a devotee whose desire is fixed in the Supreme Lord spontaneously goes to the supreme abode.

## \|\| 4.3.17 \|\|

*visesam cadarsayati \|\| 4.3.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti shows the distinction between the transcendentalists who reach the supreme abode.**

**Комментарий:**

There are two kinds of transcendentalists who take shelter of the pure form of the Supreme without resorting to symbolic worship - the devotees and the jnanis. Although their goal is one and the same, still the learned agree that there is a distinction: the former enjoy varieties of bliss, and the latter enjoy bliss without any variety. Such a distinction is shown in the sruti.

## Section 4

## \|\| 4.4.1 \|\|

*sampadyavirbhavah svena-sabdat \|\| 4.4.1 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having attained the supreme abode, one’s divine body becomes manifest by the will of Krsna, as stated in the sruti.**

**Комментарий:**

Now the divine body and enjoyment of a raganuga devotee who has attained Krsna are described, which are all caused by His desire. By Krsna's will, the transcendental body and transcendental enjoyment of a raganuga devotee who has attained Him then become manifest. So is the description found in the sruti, “Then one enjoys all pleasures accompanied by the Supreme Lord.”

## \|\| 4.4.2 \|\|

*muktah pratijnanat \|\| 4.4.2 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That person is liberated, for this is the proposed topic.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “That sruti passage is about a person enjoying, but not in the liberated stage. "It is not so. The person described is indeed liberated, for it is seen that this is the topic previously proposed to be described.

## \|\| 4.4.3 \|\|

*atma prakaranat \|\| 4.4.3 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Soul enjoys with the liberated souls, for He is the topic.**

**Комментарий:**

When it is stated that the liberated soul enjoys with the Supreme Brahman (brahmana), the word 'brahman' there means the Supreme Soul, for He is the topic under discussion.

## \|\| 4.4.4 \|\|

*avibhagena drstatvat \|\| 4.4.4 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is an unbroken connection between the sentences in that passage, for this is clearly seen there.**

**Комментарий:**

Because a description of the Supreme Lord's nature was previously seen in the passage and is followed by a statement about the enjoyment of the liberated soul, there must be an unbroken connection between both sentences, for this is clearly seen there. Therefore, Lord Hari is the 'atma' mentioned there.

## \|\| 4.4.5 \|\|

*brahmena jaiminir upanyasadibhyah \|\| 4.4.5 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Jaimini, the liberated soul enjoys with a transcendental body, for this is stated and explained in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Jaimini says that the liberated soul enjoys all pleasures with a transcendental body, for this is stated and explained in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.4.6 \|\|

*citi tan-matrena tad-atmakatvad ity audulomih \|\| 4.4.6 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Audulomi, on account of the jiva^ nature, the liberated soul is pure consciousness in the conscious Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Audulomi says that on account of the jiva’s nature, it enjoys being situated in the conscious Brahman in the form of pure consciousness.

## \|\| 4.4.7 \|\|

*evam apy upanyasat purva-bhavad avirodham badarayanah \|\| 4.4.7 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vyasadeva says that there is no contradiction, for the same is confirmed by the previous statement.**

**Комментарий:**

Vyasadeva says that there is no contradiction if the liberated souls obtain a body of pure consciousness, for this agrees with the previous statement. One should consider that the devotees obtain a transcendental body and the jndnis attain a position in oneness with the impersonal Brahman.

## \|\| 4.4.8 \|\|

*sankalpad eva tac chruteh \|\| 4.4.8 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the sruti, the devotees’ body and bliss become manifest by Lord Krsna’s will.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotees ’ divine body, bliss, enjoyment and so on are considered to become manifest by Lord Krsna's will, for this is stated in the sruti.

## \|\| 4.4.9 \|\|

*ata eva cananyadhipatih \|\| 4.4.9 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, Lord Hari is the master of those exclusively devoted souls.**

**Комментарий:**

Because Lord Hari Himself bestows everything upon the raganuga devotees, therefore He is said to be the master of those exclusively devoted souls.

## \|\| 4.4.10 \|\|

*abhavam badarir aha hy evam \|\| 4.4.10 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sage Badari states that the liberated jnanis have no body, for the sruti says so.**

**Комментарий:**

The sage Badari thinks that the jnanis who attained the impersonal Brahman have no body and senses, for the sruti says so.

## \|\| 4.4.11 \|\|

*bhavam jaiminir vikalpamananat \|\| 4.4.11 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Jaimini, the devotees have bodies, for the option is mentioned in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The sage Jaimini thinks that the devotees who attained the personal form of the Supreme Lord have bodies and senses, for the option is seen in the scriptures.

## \|\| 4.4.12 \|\|

*dvadasahavad ubhaya-vidham badarayano’tah \|\| 4.4.12 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Vyasadeva, the liberated soul can manifest a body or not according to one’s will, just as in the Dvadasaha ceremony.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “It is heard that Brahman is only one, but you consider it two, which contradicts the scriptures." In response to this, we say the following: Our view is that Brahman is only one, and not two, but according to the different meditations, it can appear as twofold to the meditators. Just like the Dvadasaha ceremony can be performed as a sacrifice with a single priest or as a sattra with multiple priests according to the will of the sacrificer. Similarly, the liberated soul can manifest a body or not according to their will.

## \|\| 4.4.13 \|\|

*tanv-abhave sandhyavad upapatteh \|\| 4.4.13 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Although Lord Krsna has no material body, a deluded person sees Him otherwise, just as what is seen in a dream, for this is logical.**

**Комментарий:**

To see something that appears material in Lord Krsna’s blissful form, although there is nothing material in it, is like something seen in a dream. Just as a sleeping person sees false objects in dreams, similarly a person bewildered by maya sees Krsna’s transcendental body as material.

## \|\| 4.4.14 \|\|

*bhave jagradvat \|\| 4.4.14 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise see Lord Krsna just as an awake person sees an existing object.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as one who is awake sees an object as it is, similarly a wise person actually sees Lord Krsna as bliss personified.

## \|\| 4.4.15 \|\|

*pradipavad avesas tatha hi darsayati \|\| 4.4.15 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord pervades His devotees like light, for the scriptures teach so.**

**Комментарий:**

Pervading them like light, the powerful Supreme Lord then makes His devotees similar to Himself in order to enjoy pleasures. So say the sruti and the Vaisnava smrti.

## \|\| 4.4.16 \|\|

*svapyaya-sampatyor anyatarapeksyam aviskrtam hi \|\| 4.4.16 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no enjoyment during sleep, and there is enjoyment in the Lord’s company, for both things are revealed by different sruti passages.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The existence of enjoyment is both denied and confirmed in the sruti. It is denied when one’s form is without any attributes, and it is confirmed when one’s form is material. Since everything is correct in such a conclusion, what need is there to explain Brahman as twofold?” Please do not speak so. The scriptures deny the existence of enjoyment during sleep and confirm it in the company of Lord Visnu, the husband of Laksmi. This is revealed by two statements in the sruti. Therefore, our conclusion is correct.

## \|\| 4.4.17 \|\|

*jagad-vyapara-varjam prakaranad asannihitatvac ca \|\| 4.4.17 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Transcendental enjoyment has nothing to do with mundane activities, for these topics are discussed in different sections and there is no proximity between them.**

**Комментарий:**

The Supreme Lord’s and the devotees’ enjoyment mentioned in the scriptures must be transcendental, for this topic is discussed in passages about the pure spiritual platform, while the enjoyment of insignificant material objects is discussed in passages very far from those.

## \|\| 4.4.18 \|\|

*pratyaksopadesad iti cen nadhikarika-mandala-sthokteh \|\| 4.4.18 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If someone objects that the sruti speaks about material sense perception after liberation, we say that this is not true, for whatever is perceived by the qualified devotees is transcendental.**

**Комментарий:**

Someone may argue, “The sruti speaks about the connection with the material world through sensorial perception - ‘sarvam ha pasyah pasyati’ (one who sees the Lord sees everything). Thus, how come the previous statement?” It is not so. What is expressed by the word 'sarva’ (everything) in this sentence is the collection of objects utilized by the qualified devotees. Therefore, what was stated above is corroborated. The devotees qualified for the Supreme Lord’s pastimes are here called "adhikarika. ’ Therefore, the objects related to them are transcendental.

## \|\| 4.4.19 \|\|

*vikaravarti ca tatha hi sthitim aha \|\| 4.4.19 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no alteration in Lord Krsna’s pastimes, for the sruti describes their transcendental position.**

**Комментарий:**

Here the pastimes of Lord Krsna are referred to by the implied word "prakrti’ (the original state). Something opposite to this is called an alteration (vikara) of it. It is concluded that in His pastimes, knowledge of union and knowledge of separation are not contradicted by each other, although they are opposite to each other. Even though it is ascertained from the scriptures that the Supreme Lord is everything everywhere and always, nevertheless, by His inconceivable potencies, there is no mutual contradiction. Therefore, a devotee experiences all rasas according to different time and circumstances. Revealing the truth about the Lord’s pastimes, the sruti so describes this transcendental position.

## \|\| 4.4.20 \|\|

*darsayatas caivam pratyaksanumane \|\| 4.4.20 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sruti and the smrti teach that the Supreme Lord’s pastimes are beyond logic.**

**Комментарий:**

The pastimes performed by the Supreme Lord cannot be comprehended by logic. The sruti and the smrti always teach this.

## \|\| 4.4.21 \|\|

*bhoga-matra-samya-lingac ca \|\| 4.4.21 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The similarity between the Supreme Lord and the jiva is only in enjoyment, for this is indicated in the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

“Pervading the devotees like light. Lord Hari makes them similar to Himself, ” - It should be known that in this statement the similarity is only with respect to enjoyment, not in all respects. Although there is no difference between the part and the whole, still the Lord’s and the jiva’s attributes are never interchanged. This has been previously stated and should never be forgotten by those who love Him.

## \|\| 4.4.22 \|\|

*anavrttih sabdad anavrttih sabdat \|\| 4.4.22 \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The liberated souls never return to the material world, for the fruti declares so.**

**Комментарий:**
